# Audit evidence

[TODO](TODO.md) owns the current queue; [BASELINE](BASELINE.md) identifies retained contracts and original findings; [RELEASE](RELEASE.md) owns outstanding operator obligations. This file records accepted outcomes, final verification and unresolved limits. Historical attempts are consolidated into exact committed references, not erased or relabelled as successes.

All historical links below pin commit `dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9` unless a different accepted candidate is explicit. They preserve the recorded commands, environments, failed attempts and review dispositions. Temporary directories, screenshots and private local logs mentioned in those records are **not durable artifacts** and are not required to retrieve these summaries. A committed report of an observation is not the raw observation itself; reproduce from maintained procedures when current proof is required.

## Phase 10 D09 #1607

**Final PR in review.** D09 [#1607](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1607), task `10-09`, ships as one PR that closes the issue. It was planned as two PRs, with the reconciliation waiting on D08, but D08 merged (`ac9a3b1a0`, [#1623](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1623)) while the first was being built, so the split had no remaining reason. The regenerated [inventory](phase-10-inventory.md) shows 0 open rows, 0 unclassified rows and 0 unused native exceptions across every packet.

**Migrated consumers.**

- `patterns/type-management-workspace`: the list is a keyboard `grid` of `row`s (the selected row is the one Tab stop; Up, Down, Home and End move; Right and Left reach a row's actions; Enter or Space selects). Its raw button is gone, `disclosure` is `ui/collapsible`, and `color-input` takes its value from the route. Consumers: Training Types, Training Views, Onboarding Templates. Focus lands on the new row after a create, on the selected row after a delete or a mobile Back, and on the detail heading when the detail replaces the list below `lg`. A resize that hides the focused pane moves focus to the visible one.
- `patterns/readiness-band`, `requirement-status-badge`, `report-chart`, `workspace-toolbar` and `brand-wordmark` paint from central tokens and utilities in `src/app.css`; their `<style>` blocks, `var(--…)` skins and hex are gone. `EvalBadge` and `TrainingStatusCell` use the new `expiring` token instead of raw orange classes.
- `ui/empty` `Empty.Title` is a heading (`level` 2, 3 or 4). `DataTable` takes `emptyLevel` and the report-workspace state takes `level`; callers under section headings pass the level that fits their outline.
- One `main` per page: `Sidebar.Inset` renders a `div`, the org shell keeps `main#main-content`, the admin layout wraps its content in a `main`, 13 page-level `main`s became `div`s, and Account, `/dashboard`, `/org/new`, `/suspended`, the root error page and the Organization suspended and session-unavailable branches gain the `main` they lacked.
- The Sign-in Roster list, Person Training editor, Availability's uncertain-save state and an exempt Training Record hide `AppDialog`'s icon button while their footer `Close` shows, so each dialog has one `Close`.
- `BannerStack` moved from `src/lib/components/` to its only owner, `src/features/app-shell/components/`; the `foundation-placement` rule now rejects a loose file directly under `src/lib/components/`.

**Deleted.** `bulk-import-source-option` and `bulk-import-source-picker` (only a test harness used them), the route-tabs button mode and its `value`/`onValueChange` props (every consumer passes `href`), the wordmark's `--public-brand-*` hooks and `titleClass`/`subtitleClass` props, `BannerStackTest.svelte`, the empty `ui/toolbar` catalog stub, and the page `main`s above.

**Native exceptions.** `scripts/ui-inventory.ts` gives every row a disposition: complete, test support (by path), native with a recorded reason, or OPEN. Each native entry records the exact candidate counts it covers, so a changed count turns the row OPEN again. The recorded reasons are the ones earlier packets established and D09 checked against source: Calendar's month `DateCell` button and its layout variables (D03), the Calendar settings color-input defaults (D03) and the Training print tables (D04). `--check` still fails only on a stale file; D10 reads the `Open rows` total from a fresh run.

**Catalog.** [`component-catalog.md`](../component-catalog.md) states the three-layer ownership, each changed family's real consumers and the wordmark API. It no longer claims migrations production does not use.

**Routed observations.** All four D06 observations are repaired: Templates (now every type list) arrow keys, the duplicate dialog `Close`, `Empty.Title` as a heading and the nested `main`. The empty roster dialog's two `New Roster` buttons (D06 observation) are consumer behavior and were not routed here.

**Accepted differences.** At exactly 640px the wordmark keeps its full size; it now switches at Tailwind's `max-sm` (below 640px), the same breakpoint as `PublicSiteNav` around it. Printing in dark theme shows Expiring in the light print ink, like the other status inks; the old class printed light orange on paper (source-reasoned, not printed).

**Runs and archives.** Every run used Chromium 152.0.7977.82 and `verify:app --worker 1` in its own checkout. The [shared compositions recipe](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/shared-compositions.md) and its `style-probe`/`style-diff` controls are new; the diff compares selector sets in both directions plus page, viewport and theme.

Each archive was re-downloaded with `gh release download` and its hash and `INVENTORY.sha256` verified.

**Results.**

- **Baseline failures, all repaired on the final runs.** Type-list arrow keys, `Empty.Title` as a `div`, two or three `main`s on Organization pages and none on Account, and the roster dialog's two `Close` controls.
- **Styling equivalence.** 92 of 92 probes compared with 0 unexpected computed differences on the readiness band, status badges, report charts, route tabs and toolbar (1280, 820 and 390). Every difference is an intended markup change (heading tags, `main`, grid rows, removed style-only attributes, chart paint moved to classes) or a recorded measurement artifact. The fix 1 re-diff with the two-way diff found 0 page, viewport or theme mismatches and no new difference. Probes cap at 200 elements per selector, so the seeded Training Reports page compared its first 200 of 475 band elements.
- **Interactions.** Grid keyboard, focus after create, delete, mobile Back and a resize across `lg`, one `Close` per dialog (uncertain-save reached through a scripted 503), one `main` on every page checked including the suspended Organization, route tabs with `aria-current`, and failed creates keeping their values all pass.
- **Empty headings.** 22 empty branches read live with fitting levels. Types, Views and Templates empty titles moved to level 3 to match their detail headers, and the ICT completion preview's to `h3` under its `h2` review heading (a test fails without it). Ranks, the admin dashboard empties, the in-component `Access Restricted`, affected-soldiers, the session-unavailable branch and an empty Calendar report result are UNREACHABLE with existing fixtures. Bulk-import preview empties are reachable with an empty or header-only file but were not exercised live. Each passes an explicit level or the primitive default that fits its nearest heading, recorded in the fix 2 audit table; DataTable, ReportWorkspace, the ICT preview and the session-unavailable branch have unit tests.
- **UNRUN.** The 390 dark dialog `Close` checks beyond the roster, the skip link and the `Empty.Title` outline at 390.

**Checks.** On `1369a7836`: `format:check`, `lint` (0 errors), `governance:check` (0 findings), `check` (0 errors, 0 warnings), the full test suite with `--maxWorkers=6` (7,772 tests), `build` and `inventory:ui -- --check` pass. Svelte autofixer reported no issue in changed components. Exact-head CI is recorded on the PR.

**Review.** CodeRabbit reviewed the committed diff against `ac9a3b1a0`. Its four findings (one-directional style diff, probe extras replacing defaults, recipe wording, grid keys inside text controls) are fixed in fix 1. Independent acceptance (a different model, artifact review) returned ACCEPT WITH FIXES on `a5dd29494`. Its blocking findings were mobile focus, the Organization fallback `main`, unobserved empty-heading callers and this record; fix 2 and this section address them. Round 2 on `d92942309` resolved those and found two more: focus left on a pane hidden by a resize, and the ICT completion preview's `h4`. Fix 3 (`b0be379ad`) repairs both. CodeRabbit found nothing in `a5dd29494..d92942309` (23 files).

## Phase 10 D08 #1606

**Accepted and merged.** D08 [#1606](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1606), task `10-08`, merged as one PR, [#1623](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1623), squashed to `ac9a3b1a0`; #1606 closed on merge. Every `10-08` inventory row reports 0 raw controls, 0 `<style>` blocks, 0 `var(--…)` and 0 hex. Open PR [#1409](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1409), a landing redesign, was neither adopted nor discarded: D08 migrated what is on main, as the issue directs, so #1409 will need a rebase onto the migrated public components if it proceeds.

**Migrated consumers.** `PublicShell`, `PublicSiteNav`, `PublicCta`, `PublicFooter`, `PublicPageHeader`, `PublicContentSection` and `PublicContactCard` in `src/lib/components/public/`, and the Home, About, Security, Privacy and Terms routes. Static prose stays semantic HTML.

**Deleted.** All ten `<style>` blocks; the parallel token sets `--brass*`, `--public-*`, `--hero-*` and `--classification-*` (PublicShell), `--about-*` (About) and `--_*` (PublicSiteNav); PublicSiteNav's `--public-brand-*` override; the hand-drawn theme icons, CTA arrow and FAQ chevron; the global `.page-meta` class (now `PublicPageHeader`'s `meta` prop).

**Central replacements.** `src/app.css` gains `--accent-text` (AA amber for small text on paper; the bright accent in dark), `--card-alt` (the alternating long-form band), `--color-accent-hover`, and the utilities `reveal`, `bg-chrome-dots` and `bg-noise`. Every other old token mapped to an existing token with an identical value, except: the light classification bar (`bg-muted`, 0.18 lightness points), the page-header meta line (its faint amber tint is gone), and dark About, which now uses the central dark palette (brighter accent, `muted-foreground` and `border`).

**Raw controls.** The theme toggle is `Button` (ghost icon, lucide Sun/Moon). The Home FAQ is `ui/accordion` (single, collapsible). Header, hero, About and closing actions are `Button href`. No native control remains.

**Shared impact.** `ui/button` gains an `accent` variant (header Request Access, `PublicCta`, the Home hero and closing actions); existing variants are unchanged. `patterns/brand-wordmark` gains `variant="on-chrome"` with its only consumer `PublicSiteNav`, as the D01 note asked; its remaining override hooks stay for D09 [#1607](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1607). The other consumer, `AuthShell`, was observed on `/auth/login` at 1280 light and dark and 390 light: default wordmark, `Back to site`, `Toggle theme` and `Sign In` render as before.

**Claims.** Terms listed "duty assignments" as a product feature; it now says "daily assignments". No pricing, plan, subscription, billing, trial, seat or checkout wording appears on any public page. Remaining matches are everyday English ("make the plan work", "rotated through") or acceptable-use text.

**Recipe.** [Public and marketing pages](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/public-pages.md) is new. The issue's "mobile menu" does not exist: below 900px the header keeps the brand, `Toggle theme`, `Sign In` and `Request Access`, and the footer carries About, Security, FAQ, Privacy Policy and Terms of Use. The pages are static, so loading, empty, error and denied states do not apply; signed out versus signed in is the only access state. After the baseline the recipe replaced class-name selectors with observable ones (scroll container, overlay style, `aria-current`, outline or shadow focus rings) and skips `aria-hidden` decoration in the contrast check; the expected results did not change.

**Runs and archives.** Every run used worker 1, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, the public-pages recipe, a signed-out isolated context and `viewer` for the signed-in step. Pages were loaded at least 3 seconds apart to stay under the 60-per-minute rate limit.

No redaction was needed; the captures contain only public page text and synthetic data.

Unchanged passes on both: header links and active state, theme persistence across reload and pages, the Home overlay header and its solid state, the 390 header and footer with no horizontal overflow, FAQ one-open with mouse, Enter and Space, seven footer links, contact `mailto:` links, keyboard order and focus rings, signed-in header and calls to action, and every Home section revealed before capture.

**Limits.** The owned headless Chromium reports `(hover: hover)` false and the DevTools tools offer no pointer or reduced-motion emulation. Tailwind wraps `hover:` styles in `@media (hover: hover)`, so no hovered colour can be observed live; observation 9 rests on the shipped rule and the resolved token. The reveal utility hides content only under `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference`; observation 11 exercises the script branch through a labelled `initScript` stub, not the media query.

**Approved visual differences.** Lucide icons (theme toggle, CTA arrow); the FAQ uses the shared accordion chevron and animation, each row about 2px taller (+12px on Home); Button and accordion focus rings are the shared ring; accent buttons lighten on hover; About buttons use the outline variant in dark; the page-header meta line loses its tint; dark About uses the central dark palette; inner pages are 2px taller from the footer; the active header link adds `aria-current="page"`.

**Shared-consumer disposition.** `ui/button` has 156 consumers in the inventory. D08 adds one variant and changes no existing variant, size or shared rendering, so existing consumers render as before; they were not each observed live. The three `accent` consumers were covered by the public runs, and `/auth/login` sampled the existing default, outline and icon variants. `patterns/brand-wordmark`'s two consumers were both observed: `PublicSiteNav` in every public run and `AuthShell` on `/auth/login`.

**Review and acceptance.** The CodeRabbit CLI reported 0 findings on `d1e056b1a..ddc7ad13e` (24 files). Independent acceptance (GPT-6.1-Sol, read-only, separate from the implementing agents) returned HOLD on `abbf7b106` with three must-fix findings: FAQ hover contrast, the IntersectionObserver fallback listening to the window, and new reveal tests that asserted classes rather than behavior. `85b4c0de4` fixed the first two (observations 9 and 10) and deleted those tests. It also asked for the shared-consumer disposition above. Round 2 on `d5150a1c3` confirmed those resolved, accepted the hover and reduced-motion limits as honest, verified all five archives (317 payload hashes) and returned HOLD on documentation only: an unformatted table row in `docs/ui-foundation-provenance.md` and two overstated record lines. `84f8a7f4c` fixed them, and round 3 returned ACCEPT on `84f8a7f4c`, subject to exact-head CI.

**Checks.** The About signed-in action, FAQ one-open and theme-toggle tests were each confirmed to fail with the behavior broken; the signed-in and signed-out destination tests cover the retained links. On `231f137d3`: `format:check`, `lint` (0 errors, 3 pre-existing warnings in untouched files), `governance:check` (0 findings), `check` (0 errors in 7,987 files) and `inventory:ui -- --check` pass. The Svelte autofixer reported no issues on the changed components apart from one optional `bind:this` suggestion, which was kept. The full test suite and production build run in CI for the exact head.

## Phase 10 D07 #1605

**Merged and complete.** D07 [#1605](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1605), task `10-07`, shipped as two PRs; PR 2 merged as [#1622](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1622) on 2026-10-11 (`d1e056b1a`) and closed the issue. The halves use separate fixture roles and each has a state matrix of about 20 screens, so one PR would be too large to review and verify. PR 1, the Organization half, merged as [#1621](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1621). PR 2, platform admin, was the final PR for the issue and completed #1605 when it merged. Independent acceptance accepted it in round 3, after HOLDs in rounds 1 and 2; its exact-head CI is recorded on the PR. The PR 1 record comes first, then PR 2, then the issue acceptance for both halves.

**PR 1, Organization half: merged.** PR 1 merged as [#1621](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1621) on 2026-10-11 and was squashed to `630eabf76`. Main's tree is identical to its accepted head `925ea1958`, which was based on main `5bc79b9d9` (D05 [#1620](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1620)). It used `Refs #1605`. Exact-head CI passed on `310eb160e`, `0f395778b` and `925ea1958`. CodeRabbit approved it after its 3 findings, all valid, were fixed. Independent acceptance round 3 returned ACCEPT. PR 1 covers Organization settings (General, Members, Policies, Approvals, Logs, Exports and Danger), `/org/new`, the Dashboard Organization picker, the announcement banner and Send Feedback. Ranks and Archives stay with their own recipes, `rank-settings.md` and `personnel-form-states.md`.

**Runs and archives.** Every run used worker 1, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark (theme through the visible `Toggle theme`) and the [Organization settings states](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/organization-settings-states.md) recipe at its tested revision. Actors: `orgOwner`, `orgAdmin`, `orgMember`, `orgSettingsPeer`, `orgSettingsSwitch`, `orgInvitee`, `orgCreator`, `deletionRequester`, `notificationMember` and `notificationOther`. Failure cases used the synthetic 503 from `network arm-fail` and, from the acceptance run on, the synthetic 500 from `arm-error`. Busy cases used `arm-hold`. Every run ended with its controls disarmed and its provider disposed. In the final run, the helper refused `clean-seeds` on the stale first launch, and `verify:app restart` disposed it. The revisions before `73300e117` are pre-rebase commits. `git patch-id` shows each one's branch diff equal, file by file, to its rebased commit's diff, except the regenerated `phase-10-inventory.md`.

On 2026-10-11 each archive was downloaded again with `gh release download evidence -p <archive> -D DIR` into its own fresh directory. The repository is private, so the plain release URL returns `Not Found` without authentication. All six archive hashes matched (the two refresh archives were checked the same way after upload), every `INVENTORY.sha256` entry matched, and each extracted file set equaled its inventory. To verify, extract with `tar -xzf` into an empty directory and run `sha256sum -c INVENTORY.sha256` in `1605-org-<run>/`. Each `NOTES.md` holds the run log. The only redaction is the checkout path in `cleanup.json`, written as `<checkout>`. The text captures hold no credential, and the export downloads hold synthetic fixture data only.

Adjacent checks in the final run passed: role tabs and denials for `orgAdmin`, `orgMember` and `orgSettingsPeer`, the route-tabs consumers `Account sections` and `Personnel detail sections`, and one valid `/org/new` submit showing `Creating...`. No `/admin` page renders `PageShell.Tabs`.

**Later runs.**

- **Fix run.** The final run found that on a direct load at 390 a far-right current tab (Exports, Danger) started off-screen. `route-tabs` now scrolls the current tab into view inside its own strip. Direct loads and client navigation at 390 and 1280 pass with page `scrollY` 0, as do Account sections and Personnel detail sections (`verify-baker/archived`). The run has no live pre-fix reading. The component test fails on the pre-fix component.
- **Accept run.** It answers acceptance round 1. With `arm-error`, every migrated handler keeps the page, shows the Alert in its form, section, row, dialog or sheet, keeps the typed value and returns focus to the initiating control. Banner, Transfer and Delete failures show inside the banner or dialog. The coverage matrix in its `NOTES.md` covers loading, empty, section-load error, denied, disabled, failed save and success focus for each screen. The Dashboard filter, Logs paging and Danger with no transfer candidate pass live there, after earlier runs marked them UNAVAILABLE. The final run's `NOTES.md` wrongly listed Danger with no candidate as reused from the baseline, and this run corrects it. Policies and Approvals denial are inferred from the shared settings gate. The run left two residuals, both fixed in the busy run. A disabled busy submit dropped focus to `body`, and a held `/org/new` retry briefly showed the earlier failure. It also inferred a third, a nested live region in the banner, which accept 2 fixes.
- **Busy run.** All 31 held captures kept focus on the pressed control as `aria-disabled="true"`, and a repeat click and Enter sent no second request. Success focus returns to the control, its row's Manage button, the `Organization members`, `Approval Requests` or `Available workspaces` heading, or the feedback entry (`Send feedback`, `Account menu` at 390). Transfer and Delete success redirect, and focus starts on `body` on the new page. The `/org/new` retry after a failure and an unexpected error shows no stale Alert while held.
- **Accept 2 run.** It answers acceptance round 2. With a Resend held, Invite is `aria-disabled` and sends nothing, and on release no Alert appears in the Invite form. A response that arrives after the user opens another section changes nothing. After a rejected empty name and an unexpected error, `/org/new` shows only the request error. The banner is the only `role="alert"`, and Dismiss is described by its error. At 390 the access sheet's focused Save and its Alert are both in view. Confirm reads `Confirm denial of <description>`. Delete success on a created Organization lands on `/dashboard?show=all`, and the readback finds 0 rows.

**Shared changes.**

- `patterns/page-shell/route-tabs` shows the app focus ring, names badge counts with their context and reveals the current tab inside its strip. Its consumers are Organization settings, Account sections and Personnel detail sections, all verified live.
- The root `src/routes/+error.svelte` adds `Try again` (a reload) beside `Go Home` for 5xx. A failed `__data.json` request renders this page.
- The app shell's `main#main-content` takes `tabindex="-1"`, so focus can move there after the last banner closes.
- `ui/button` adds `aria-disabled:opacity-50`. An earlier branch commit also added `aria-disabled:pointer-events-none`, so a second press fell through to `main`. `be96e9602` removed it. On main, the only Buttons with `aria-disabled` are the two `Coming soon` buttons, which are also natively disabled, and disabled href Buttons. A disabled href Button drops its href and sets `aria-disabled`, so it now also renders at 50% opacity (read from source).
- New `src/lib/utils/settleAction.ts` settles a `use:enhance` result in place. An unexpected error becomes an in-context message instead of the error page. It checks `isCurrent` before applying the result and again after each await, so a stale result changes nothing. `settled` receives the message in a `finally`, so it runs even when `update` rejects. Focus then moves to the first usable candidate, scrolled into view.

**Deleted.** `SettingsActivityRail.svelte`, replaced by `ApprovalRequestsSection.svelte`. The `/org/new` route `<style>` block and its 25 local token references. The unused approvals `auditLog` load. A route-tabs test that asserted fabricated geometry, and assertions on incidental copy.

**Decisions.** A failure stays in context as an Alert, and entered values stay. Focus never drops to `body` during or after an in-page action. Row actions name their item. Errors are tied to their field. A redirect to another page (Danger Transfer and Delete, `/org/new` Create) keeps SvelteKit's navigation focus reset and route announcer, which is the app-wide navigation contract. Acceptance round 2's redirect-focus finding was dismissed on that ground.

**Unreachable or UNRUN.** The `/org/new` unconfirmed-email notice is UNAVAILABLE. Local GoTrue refuses password sign-in for an unconfirmed email, the admin API does not clear `email_confirmed_at`, and a component test covers the notice. Locally there is no mail service, so a successful Resend toasts the delivery-failed copy. The hold control cannot fail a held Resend, so the Invite error placement during that race rests on a regression test. Accept 2 reused the custom URL set and reset from the accept run, because only its toast gate changed and a component test covers it. The accept run's confirmed Delete hit the recent-authentication gate (`/auth/login?reauth=1`). The busy and accept 2 runs completed Transfer and Delete instead. The busy run drove Approve, Accept and Decline at one viewport each, because each pair shares one handler. The baseline did not drive busy and failure states at 390, and later runs covered them.

**Observations.** Not judged against an item. The app shell renders a `main` inside a `main`, which belongs to D09 [#1607](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1607). The select listboxes have no accessible name. The Members filter is not kept in the URL. On plain `/dashboard`, declining the last invitation reloads into the existing `/org/new` redirect. While a Decline is held, Accept reads `Accepting...`. Both behaviors predate this branch.

**Operator errors and corrections.** None changed an observed result.

- Baseline. A released hold was not restored, so a warning save of 181 went through. It was restored to 180 through the same Save. The Members access restore left the same effective access but dropped the explicit `personnel: view` override key.
- Busy run. The network controls match only Organization A's endpoints. At 1280 the held Delete deleted the synthetic Organization A on release. At 390 a Delete on an `orgCreator` Organization ran unheld, and its capture was discarded. Both Organizations existed only on the disposable worker.
- Accept run. Frames with a wrong radio choice and a theme toggled on the error page were discarded and retaken. Sign-ins hit the app's auth rate limit, and the run waited for the window.
- Accept 2 `NOTES.md` says the branch was rebased onto origin/main at `5c0c9dae9`. That commit is the branch's earlier head. Main was `5bc79b9d9`.

**Reviews.** CodeRabbit CLI found 0 issues in `c7f87a53d..016a0d7bc` (42 files) and in `016a0d7bc..9fa3c08d8` (35 files). It found 0 in the round-3 commits `5c0c9dae9..73300e117` (17 files). Independent acceptance by gpt-6.1-sol reviewed read-only, separately from the implementing and driving agents.

- Round 1 on `016a0d7bc` returned HOLD. It named the error and focus handling of `use:enhance`, toast-only failures, unobserved states and tests bound to implementation. The accept run covers the fixes.
- Round 2 on `9fa3c08d8` returned HOLD. Its redirect-focus finding was dismissed (see Decisions). It also named the invitation race, the settle lifetime guards, the stale `/org/new` error, the nested alerts, focus moving before the Alert renders and the Confirm name. `5d66f4c3e..b81f88e34` fixed all of them, and the accept 2 run covers them.
- Round 3 on `73300e117` returned ACCEPT with no blocking finding. Its one should-fix: when `update()` rejects after a successful action, `settleAction` released busy state but skipped focus and rethrew, and callers still ran success-only effects. `762c5f841` returns a reload message for the in-context Alert, restores focus and gates success effects (closing the access sheet, clearing the invite email, the Dashboard success message, closing the transfer confirmation) on no error.
- The live check of that fix (refresh run below) found that a failed page-data refresh does not reject `update()`: SvelteKit renders the root error page with the action's 200 status, headed `200` with only `Go Home`. `ed4a56281` makes the root error page treat only a real 4xx as final; any other status is headed `Error` and offers `Try again`. The `settleAction` rejection path remains a guard at the framework boundary, covered by tests.

**Hosted review on [#1621](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1621).** CodeRabbit raised 3 findings on `310eb160e`, all valid: the creation recipe forbade the valid submissions its busy, retry and filter rows need, and a previous invitation failure (Dashboard response, Members Resend) stayed visible while the next request was pending. `0f395778b` supersedes the shared form result when an invitation request starts, as `/org/new` does, and corrects the recipe. Each code fix has a test that fails without it, and the review run above observed both live. CodeRabbit approved `0f395778b` and resolved its threads. Exact-head CI passed all checks on `310eb160e`, `0f395778b` and the accepted head `925ea1958`, which adds only this record. Accept cannot be failed on release (the Dashboard controls have no `arm-hold-fail`), so the held-then-failed Accept path is covered by the component test only.

**Checks on `ed4a56281`.** `format:check`, `lint` (0 errors, 3 pre-existing warnings in untouched files), `governance:check` (0 findings), `check` (0 errors, 0 warnings, 7,980 files), `VITEST_MAX_WORKERS=2 npm test` (763 files, 7,681 tests), `inventory:ui -- --check`, the documents check and `build` pass. One `build` was killed for memory while it ran right after the test suite; run alone it passed. Earlier, one default parallel `npm test` run timed out in `scripts/module-boundaries.test.ts` under load; that file passes alone.

**PR 2, platform admin: in review.** PR 2 is on branch `stabilization/1605-platform-admin`, rebased onto main `630eabf76`; its last application commit is `d462a17f9`, and the record commits follow it. It covers the admin shell (sidebar and global search), Dashboard, Users, User detail, Organizations, Organization detail, Access Requests, Feedback, Announcements and Audit Log, and the Suspend, Transfer Ownership and Delete announcement confirmations. The new [platform admin states](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/platform-admin-states.md) recipe drives them. Exact-head CI is recorded on the PR.

**PR 2 runs and archives.** Every run used worker 1, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark (theme through the visible `Toggle theme`) and the recipe at its tested revision. Actors: `platformReviewer` (super admin), `platformSupport`, `platformBilling`, `platformInactive`, `orgMember` and `notificationMember`. Failure cases used the synthetic 503 from `network arm-fail`. From the final run on, `arm-load-error` failed a page read as a server-load error, and from the accept run on, `arm-fail-400` rejected a form save. Busy cases used `arm-hold` and `arm-hold-fail`. Every run restored its controls and ended with a final doctor and `verify:app cleanup`, each with PASS. Every session ran `clean-seeds` except accept candidates A and B, where the helper refused it and the next restart disposed of their providers with their seeds. The revisions before `9fcec92e3` are pre-rebase commits. Each one's commit diff has the same `git patch-id --stable` as its rebased commit, and `git range-diff` finds all 41 admin commits identical. The tree of `fd740d57e`, the head rebased onto PR 1's `925ea1958`, equals the tree of `9fcec92e3`.

On 2026-10-11 each of the seven archives was downloaded with `gh release download evidence -R waffleflopper/trooptotask -p <archive> -D DIR` into its own fresh directory. All seven archive hashes matched, every `INVENTORY.sha256` entry matched, and each extracted file set equaled its inventory. To verify, extract with `tar -xzf` into an empty directory and run `sha256sum -c INVENTORY.sha256` in `1605-admin-<run>/`. The fix archive has no top-level directory, so run the check in the extraction directory. Each `NOTES.md` holds the run log. The checkout path in each `cleanup.json` and in the baseline's `narrow/narrow.sh` is written as `<checkout>`, and the baseline's home directory as `<home>`. A search of the extracted files found no other home path, no token-shaped string and only `example.test` addresses. The baseline's `roles.txt` names the session key fields as devalue indices only, with no token values.

The final run also showed the Dashboard signup data as a canonical table with column headers, the Feedback heading and title `Feedback`, a `Feedback saved` toast, and Inspect, Approve, Reject and Send Setup names that include their item. Its adjacent checks passed. `platformSupport` on `/admin/audit` and `platformBilling` on `/admin/users` get the admin 403 inside the admin navigation with `Back to Dashboard` and no `Try again`. `orgMember` on `/admin` gets the site-wide 403 with `Go Home`. Access Requests and Feedback tabs move with the arrow keys. Users search and its empty state, and the Organizations labels and links, pass. Suspend User, Suspend (Organization) and Transfer Ownership open with focus inside, and Escape returns focus to the opener. `arm-fail` on a page read still renders the site-wide 503, because SvelteKit renders the root page when the `__data.json` request itself fails.

**PR 2 later runs.**

- **Fix run.** It answers items 19 to 21. Two earlier candidates failed and are kept as history. On `b4129086` (`c81070b3f`), a Feedback save that moved the item to another tab left focus on `body`, because both the pressed button and the item's trigger were gone or in a hidden panel. `17527e135` fixed it. On `639beb97` (`17527e135`), a failed Feedback save left focus on `body`. Chrome keeps a focused button as `document.activeElement` after it disables, so the guard that checks whether focus already moved returned early. `4611ea158` counts a disabled or detached active element as lost focus. On the final candidate, the Announcements toggle and a failed Access Requests Reject, both consumers of the changed focus function, keep focus at both viewports.
- **Accept run.** It answers acceptance round 1. Candidates A `f9ad12742` (`dbf470867`), B `9a6f46fec` (`e71626b00`) and C `5d322ce4c` (`e6f444ab9`) ran before the final head, and sessions D, G and T ran on it. Its `NOTES.md` lists, for each of A to C, the files that changed after it and why its cells still hold. The matrix covers loading, empty, error, denied, disabled and failed save for each of 13 rows: shell and search, the nine pages, SuspendModal, TransferOwnershipModal and the Delete ConfirmDialog. It reuses cells from the final, fix and baseline archives where the owning files did not change. Account email failures, both the provider's `Failed to send password reset` and a transport error, stay inline with focus on the button, and the page never swaps to the error page. Escape during a held lookup keeps the search list closed after release. Before the fix, the Dashboard alert link overlapped its text at 768 on C. After the fix, it sits below the text at 390, 768 and 1280 on D. `Reload Feedback` and `Reload Announcements` sit below their warning text. `arm-fail-400` keeps `Save Notes` enabled and focused with no reload offer. A failed User or Organization detail read shows the admin 503 with `Try again`, which recovers.
- **Settle run.** It answers the move of every admin `use:enhance` handler onto `settleAction`. Announcements create, toggle and delete, `Reload Announcements`, Feedback `Save Notes`, `Reload Feedback`, Access review, account email, and Suspend and Transfer confirms pass their success, failure and held cells at both viewports, except the cells listed as unavailable or reused. A held control keeps focus with `aria-disabled` and `aria-busy`, and a second press adds no request. A held Suspend or Transfer confirm keeps focus, the dialog is `aria-busy` and Escape is ignored. The admin error page shows `Try again` for a 503 and not for `platformBilling`'s 403 on `/admin/users`.
- **Accept 2 run.** It answers acceptance round 2. Users and Organizations keep focus on the input after Enter and on Apply after a click, keep the query and reset to page 1. A `seed organization-pages` Organization shows `No members found.` at both viewports. A 60-character owner email wraps within its column at 1280, and at 390 the table scrolls inside its card with the page 390 wide.
- **Paging run.** It answers a defect the accept 2 worker found: a page past the last result (`/admin/organizations?q=zzz&page=2`, `/admin/audit?page=99`) rendered the admin 500 page because the provider rejects a row range past the last row. `d462a17f9` runs a count-only query on that error and re-reads the last page, so each directory clamps to its last page; Users, which had no provider range, now reports a truthful page. Seven tests at the owning layer fail without the fix.

**PR 2 shared changes.** No `ui` or `patterns` family changed.

- `src/lib/utils/settleAction.ts` exports `restoreFocus`, which `settleAction` now calls. It focuses the first usable candidate and moves to the next one when a candidate refuses focus, such as a control in a hidden tab panel. Its consumers are PR 1's Organization settings Danger and General, Members, the Dashboard and `/org/new`, and the admin Access Requests, Announcements, Feedback and User detail pages. For PR 1's consumers the result differs only when a candidate refuses focus. `settleAction.test.ts` covers that case, and no run re-drove PR 1's screens after the change.
- New `src/routes/admin/+error.svelte` renders inside the admin layout. It heads any status of 400 or more with that number, and anything below 400 (a failed client-side refresh) with `Error`. It offers `Try again` below 400 and at 500 or more, never for a real 4xx. This is the same rule as PR 1's root error page. A non-admin denied by the admin layout still reaches the root 403.
- New `AdminPager` pages Users, Organizations and Audit Log and keeps focus on Previous or Next. It is in the platform-admin public list in `scripts/module-boundaries.json`.
- The verification helpers add `arm-load-error`, `arm-fail-400` and the `organization-pages` seed, each with tests.

**PR 2 deleted.** The Dashboard's raw signup `table`, now the canonical Table. The Audit Log raw `details`, and the Feedback raw card button, both now Collapsible. The native `window.confirm` for announcement Delete. The second search element and the focusable brand and account buttons. The admin `restoreSubmitterFocus` helper, added and then deleted on the branch, so it leaves no trace against main.

**PR 2 decisions.** PR 1's decisions apply: a failure stays in context as an Alert, entered values stay, and focus never drops to `body` during or after an in-page action. A busy control uses `aria-disabled` and `aria-busy` instead of `disabled`, so it keeps focus. An admitted platform admin denied one admin page sees the admin 403 inside the admin layout with navigation filtered to their role. Billing sees Dashboard, and support sees Dashboard, Users, Organizations, Access Requests and Feedback. A non-admin gets the site-wide 403. Acceptance round 1's denied-page admin chrome finding was dismissed on that ground.

**PR 2 unreachable or UNRUN.** These cells are UNAVAILABLE live, and component tests cover each one:

- `Send Password Reset` success. There is no local mail service, so the real send returns `Failed to send password reset`.
- `Resend Invite`. The fixture creates every account with its email confirmed, so the button never renders.
- The in-page `Reload requests` retry. A failed re-read renders the admin error page, so the in-page retry never shows enabled. A held re-read shows it disabled and busy.
- Dashboard empty. The fixture always has accounts and audit events.

The accept run also marked Organization detail `No members found.` UNAVAILABLE. Acceptance round 2 found that cell reachable, and the accept 2 run observed it at both viewports. The final run drove the denied checks at 1280 only, and the accept run's G session drove them at both viewports.

**PR 2 observations.** Not judged against an item. A desktop sidebar link choice leaves focus on `body`, SvelteKit's navigation reset, as at baseline. Eleven SVG `<marker>` elements in the shared report chart carry `id=""`, outside this packet. The admin error heading is the status number. At baseline, `Toggle Sidebar` exposed no `aria-expanded`, and no later run checked it. Transfer Ownership's disabled confirm has no description of why it is disabled (read from source).

**PR 2 operator errors and corrections.** None changed an observed result. Each real write happened on the disposable worker's synthetic data and was reversed or cleaned.

- Baseline. The native `window.confirm` blocked the verification tool until its 120 s timeout. `c92dacc70` (pre-rebase `145148efe`) records the gotcha in the recipe after the run. A click preloaded a page on hover and used up the armed failure, so later steps used keyboard Enter.
- Final. A second network arm was refused, and the next Enter rejected the seeded reject request for real. `access/1280-platform-state-*-stray-reject.json` records it. The 1280 unchanged-state proof was redone with a failed resend, and `clean-seeds` removed the request and both audit rows.
- Fix. One read-only `verify:app doctor` ran from the coordinator's worktree. Its output was discarded and the check redone.
- Accept. On A and B, the helper refused `clean-seeds` with `STALE_CANDIDATE` because files changed first. The next restart disposed of those providers with their seeds. In G, three 390 holds first focused a link behind the open sheet and were rerun. The 1280 Approve first hit the recent sign-in guard with no state change. In T, `arm-hold-fail admin-transfer` is not supported, and an unchecked click made a real ownership transfer on the seeded Organization. Ownership was transferred back through the UI, and `clean-seeds` then deleted the Organization and its memberships.
- Settle. Driver mistakes stopped the first 1280 Announcements and Feedback attempts. The created announcement was deleted through the UI, and the Feedback row was set back to New before the scenarios reran. A `wait moved` check fails in both Feedback runs, because a moved item's editor stays in the hidden tab panel. The next check, focus on the active tab, passes. The first held Transfer ended at the step-up sign-in with no ownership write and was rerun after `reauth`. The deliberate Transfer round trip leaves `orgMember` as admin, and `clean-seeds` removed the Organization.

**PR 2 reviews.** CodeRabbit CLI found 0 issues on main..`526b4f225` and on the full rebased admin diff (43 files). A coordinator review found items 19 to 21, and the fix run covers them. Independent acceptance by GPT-6.1-Sol reviewed read-only, separately from the implementing and driving agents.

- Round 1 on `526b4f225` (`4611ea158`) returned HOLD. It named the account email `update()` and error handling, the `Alert.Action` overlap at 390, Escape during a pending `AdminSearch` lookup, coverage gaps and tests bound to implementation. Its denied-page admin chrome finding was dismissed (see Decisions). The accept run covers the fixes. The settle work followed: every admin `use:enhance` handler moved onto `settleAction`, `restoreSubmitterFocus` was deleted, and `settleAction` gained `restoreFocus`.
- Round 2 on `9fcec92e3` returned HOLD. It named filter Apply focus on Users and Organizations, the Organization detail empty-members cell recorded as unavailable when it is reachable, and long owner emails that overlap. `8745b7060` fixed all three, and the accept 2 run covers them. The paging defect found during that run is fixed in `d462a17f9`.
- Round 3 on `a4e702921` returned ACCEPT with no blocking finding. It confirmed the round 2 fixes in the accept 2 captures, and that the paging fix takes the count fallback only on `PGRST103`, uses the same filters for both queries and reads at most one bounded extra page. It verified all 16 archives. Its two should-fix findings corrected this record's wording on the error-page headings and on which repairs have automated tests.

**PR 2 checks on `fd740d57e`.** `format:check`, `lint`, `governance:check` (0 findings), `check` (0 errors), `VITEST_MAX_WORKERS=2 npm test` (767 files, 7,727 tests), `inventory:ui -- --check` and `build`, run alone, pass. The tree of `fd740d57e` equals the tree of `9fcec92e3`. On `a4e702921`, after the paging fix: `format:check`, `lint` (0 errors, 3 pre-existing warnings in untouched files), `governance:check` (0 findings), `check` (0 errors, 7,986 files), `VITEST_MAX_WORKERS=2 npm test` (767 files, 7,737 tests), `inventory:ui -- --check` and `build`, run alone, pass. CodeRabbit CLI found 0 issues in the full rebased diff (43 files) and 2 in `9fcec92e3..a4e702921`, both corrections to this record (the archive count and the stated head), fixed here.

**Issue acceptance.** #1605 is complete when PR 2 passes acceptance round 3 and exact-head CI and merges. PR 1 meets its share on main.

- **Migrated consumers, deletions, interactions and shared-consumer review.** PR 1's record above and [#1621](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1621) cover the Organization half. PR 2's record above covers platform admin, and its PR body lists the migrated consumers and deletions.
- **Inventory.** On PR 2's head, `inventory:ui` reports packet `10-07` at 39 rows, 0 with candidates, 0 raw controls, 0 `<style>` blocks, 0 variables and 0 hex values. Main reported 37 rows with 3 raw controls after PR 1.
- **Named observations.** PR 1 deleted the `/org/new` `<style>` block and its 25 token references. PR 2 replaced the admin overview `table`, the audit `details` and the feedback raw button. Members, Approvals, Policies, Danger and the Transfer and Suspend dialogs keep their permission-derived states and confirmations, observed in both halves' runs.
- **Component tests and Definition of Done.** Interaction, state and paging repairs have behavioral tests that fail without their fix. Visual repairs (wrapping and overlap at 390 and 1280) have captured visual evidence instead of geometry assertions, as [Testing strategy](../architecture/TESTING.md) directs. PR 1 passed the full sequence on `ed4a56281` and PR 2 on `fd740d57e`. The coordinator ran the Svelte autofixer on all 14 changed admin components at `e51e8d1cf`: 11 are clean; Announcements and Feedback suggest reviewing `retirePage` inside an `$effect`, code already on main since #1516 that intentionally resets page state when the signed-in admin changes; `AdminPager` flags `goto()` without `resolve()`, a false positive because every caller builds `pageHref` with `resolve()`. PR 1's implementing workers reported the autofixer clean on its changed components; no autofixer output is archived for PR 1.
- **Exact-head CI.** PR 1 passed on `925ea1958`. PR 2's is recorded on its PR.
- **Affected journeys.** PR 1's runs repeat all 33 baseline items, and PR 2's runs repeat all 18 plus items 19 to 21. Each candidate matches its baseline except for the repairs above. The accept 2 and paging runs cover the acceptance round 2 fixes and the paging defect.
- **Shared-consumer review.** PR 1 verified every route-tabs consumer live, the root error page, the `main` focus target, `ui/button`'s `aria-disabled` styling and `settleAction`. PR 2 changes no `ui` or `patterns` family. Its `settleAction` change is covered for PR 1's consumers by test, as stated under PR 2 shared changes.

## Phase 10 D05 #1603

**Unchanged-application baseline (verification preparation; no application change).** D05 [#1603](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1603): Critical Tasks library, person detail, matrix and reports, and Evals, on the canonical UI foundation. Recipes, one control allowlist entry and the unchanged-app baseline only. Tested revision `5722aa9c5` (main, application source unchanged), worker 1, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, in two launches of the same revision. The first ran candidate digest `54e6a0f3` (`54e6a0f3f9faa6c37650be2d58565d9a30630f3689e18054f681c88b0abfeb00`) at origin `http://127.0.0.1:42833`. After `ict_tasks` joined the `server-source` allowlist, `verify:app restart` ran candidate digest `69ad5df3` (`69ad5df3144892ba921469efe5ea797bc3697a148605825836dc36732ecbb414`) at origin `http://127.0.0.1:35533` for M3, M4 and L7 only. Actors: `ictOperator`, `ictConfigurator`, `ictViewer`, `ictEmpty`, `ictForeign`, `rosterManager`, `orgOwner` (Members change for L5, restored), `ratingOperate`, `ratingView`, `ratingEmpty`, `manager` and `ratingForeign`. Controls: `seed training-pages` and `seed rating-scheme`, `network arm-fail|arm-hold` on `personnel-ict-completions`, `personnel-ict-completions/batch`, `critical-tasks/matrix/export`, `critical-tasks/reports/summary` and `rating-scheme`, `server-source hold|fail-hard` on `ict_tasks` and `rating_scheme_entries`, `feedback`, `rows` and `clean-seeds`. Failure steps used the synthetic 503 from `network arm-fail` and the synthetic 500 from `server-source fail-hard`, which prove client recovery only. `clean-seeds` and the final doctor passed on both candidates, and owned cleanup passed.

They cover interactions, permission states, persistence, URL state, keyboard and focus, both viewports and themes, and loading, empty, error and disabled states. The workspace reads completions through the `latest_active_personnel_ict_completions` RPC, which `server-source` cannot match, so `ict_tasks` (the workspace's catalog GET) joined `SERVER_SOURCE_TABLES` with a test, and M3, M4 and L7 name it. **Unreachable or UNRUN.** 390 dark was not driven for the completion dialogs (M7–M10), export failure (M6), empty and denied states (M5), the library forms and import (L2–L7), the Evals modal states (E3–E6) and the streamed-source rows. M15 empty (`ictEmpty` on Reports) and the M10 retry were not driven; recipe step 4 owns the import retry. Actors: `ictOperator`, `ictConfigurator`, `ictViewer`, `ictEmpty`, `ictForeign`, `rosterManager`, `orgOwner` (L5 Members change, restored, and the consumer sweep), `ratingOperate`, `ratingView`, `ratingEmpty`, `manager` and `ratingForeign`. **Failing results.** L6 fails its recipe row. The task import is an inline region, so the row's dialog and Escape clause cannot pass. Reproduce with `ictConfigurator` on the library at 1280: choose `Import CSV`, upload one valid and one invalid row, then press Escape. Expected: the import closes and focus returns to `Import CSV`. Observed: nothing happens and focus stays on `Review Import` (`library/L6-*`). Issue #1603 does not require a dialog. If the inline workspace is the accepted design, the recipe row should read: focus moves into the import workspace on open and returns to `Import CSV` on Cancel. That edit was not made, because it changes the expectation and is the coordinator's call. Three pre-existing focus losses outside D05's changed code also fail the sweep's focus clause: self-disabling create buttons (Onboarding templates, Training types and views) and Calendar bulk tab switching.

**L6 disposition.** L6: the dialog clause was the baseline author's assumption, not a contract; the inline workspace is retained and the row now states its focus contract.

**Other observations.** Not judged against a row. Evals `Export` drops focus to `body` because the button disables itself while exporting; the wiring is unchanged since `5722aa9c5`. After a completion import finishes, focus moves to the dialog container. After `New Task`, focus is on `body`. A blocked Calendar import row's accessible name omits the reason, which its description carries. Wide non-D05 tables at 390 scroll in an unnamed container that cannot take focus. Combobox and select options are missing from the native accessibility snapshot. The matrix workbook filename uses the UTC date. Archived personnel's Actions header is right-aligned while its cell computes left. Driver slips were caught and redone: a search field cleared with `fill ""` (real keys clear it), one accidental task move (restored), one stray Evals Export click, a wrong radio selector, and a Calendar reports pass taken before `Run`.

**Not rerun.** Baseline PASS paths the final did not repeat: M7, M8 and M9 at 1280, because dialog focus and save handling do not depend on width and the final drove 390. The M10 failure alert at 1280, because the final drove the same dialog at 390. M13 and M2 at 390, because the fallback tables are visually hidden and landmark count does not depend on width. E3 and E4 at 390, because E8 covers the modal layout at 390. L2 to L8 at 390, because L10 covers the 390 form layout. The baseline's other observations (date picker caption, combobox value names, Histogram tick overlap) were not judged again. The M10 import retry is still owned by recipe step 4.

**Rerun failing result.** The Training bulk importer does not move focus to its review heading. Reproduce with `orgOwner` on Training at 390 dark. Open `More training actions`, choose `Import` and upload a CSV with one valid row and one unknown-person row. Expected: focus on the `Preview` heading. The Critical Tasks completion import uses the same `AppDialog`. There, focus first parks on the dialog container and then moves to the heading (`critical-tasks/M10-focuslog.json`). Inferred from those two logs and not traced in source: the Training dialog's focus trap moves focus to the first tabbable control before the workspace root checks for lost focus. The final run recorded the same landing (`Select all import rows`) as a PASS. Focus never reaches `body`.

Three times, the first sidebar click right after landing on Home did not navigate, and the second click did. Driver slips were caught and redone. One expiration attempt read UIDs from the wrong snapshot file and did nothing. One upload matched a static text node and dropped focus, so L6 restarted from Cancel. One archive confirmation opened on `VERIFY-ICT-ALPHA` and was cancelled with Escape, leaving rows unchanged.

The expiration change applies only when the input reports `badInput`; other values take the unchanged parse path. The `Clear filters` change adds focus after the existing reset. The bulk-import root change reaches all five consumers, and each was driven above. Its focus steps do not depend on width, so M10 at 390 is reused. Actors, fixtures and runtime settings match the earlier runs.

**Round 4 UNRUN.** Native Back during the toolbar's close animation could not be produced. The verify tool server runs calls one at a time, and a click returns only after its navigation settles. The teardown case rests on the `WorkspaceToolbar` component test that `c4c11434d` added. A committed Personnel or Training import, and the Training import failure, were not driven. The brief excludes committing a Personnel import. Their result steps rest on the `BulkImportWorkspace` component tests that `647eca29a` updated. A held import with focus moved outside was driven on the Calendar import only. The Training and completion imports are modal dialogs, so focus cannot leave them, and Personnel has no hold control that avoids a commit.

**Matrix scroll reset check.** Tested revision `1830b4ea0` (`1830b4ea06b1badd1ec603b7820d94ca21304b0a`, branch `refactor/stabilize-1603`, clean tree), worker 1, verify:app candidate digest `05d039c4` (`05d039c46a9961f9d314a97732fe61d1bb4946d2677d66cdd4570c6c275ef124`), origin `http://127.0.0.1:39845`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, native DevTools tools. `910fd76bb` resets the matrix scroll offset while its empty state shows, and `1830b4ea0` changes only a test. Actors `ictOperator` (matrix) and `ictEmpty` (M5), both in Organization A, with `seed training-pages` (26 extra Alpha People) for the matrix and `clean-seeds` before the `ictEmpty` read. Steps, in A-Z view: scroll the matrix viewport to `scrollTop` 1500 (set by `evaluate_script`), type `zzzznomatch` into the search, then clear the search with native keys and read the viewport with no further scroll. At 1280x800 light the viewport scrolled to 1500 of 2391 and rendered `PFC MapTraining1d11321d16` first, with Able out of the window. The search showed `No personnel match the current filters.` with no viewport. After the clear the viewport read `scrollTop` 0, 11 rows, first row `SPC Able, Synthetic` and first control `Able VERIFY-ICT-ALPHA: current ... Completed 2026-09-14`. PASS. At 390x844 dark the same steps gave 1500 of 2391, empty state, then `scrollTop` 0, 10 rows, first row `SPC Able, Synthetic` and the same control. PASS. M1 held at both widths: page bottom equals the frame bottom, the document does not scroll, and after a scroll to 375 the header row sits 1px inside the panel top and the Personnel column 1px inside its left. At 1280 the matrix has no horizontal overflow and at 390 only 5px (`scrollLeft` 5), so the sticky column was read with little sideways travel. M5 `ictEmpty` read `No personnel yet.` with `Record Completion` disabled and `Add personnel and active tasks before recording a completion.` at both widths. A `fill` with an empty value left the input empty but fired no input event and the filter stayed, so the clear used a native key press. The final doctor passed on the same digest and revision, and owned cleanup returned `PASS`, phase `cleaned`. Archive [`1603-rerun3.tar.gz`](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/releases/download/evidence/1603-rerun3.tar.gz), SHA-256 `bda9b05265b9ca3c83ea64b48c8031281e92b087960c503c79fc86c277dba6a0`, 43 payload files plus `INVENTORY.sha256`, re-downloaded with `gh release download` into fresh scratch, with the archive hash, every inventory hash and the exact file set verified. `NOTES.md` holds the per-step log. Every other result is reused, because `910fd76bb` and `1830b4ea0` change only `IctMatrix.svelte` and its tests, and no other D05 surface renders the matrix viewport.

**Independent acceptance and CodeRabbit.** gpt-6.1-sol reviewed the complete D05 diff read-only in four rounds, separately from the implementing and driving agents.

- Round 1, on `f1cf70912`, requested six changes: numeric validation, the completion hydration race, toolbar deferral on unmount, module-level bulk-import focus state, L6, and a prose-bound test. `2fad89209..4595b7700` fixed all six.
- Round 2, on `4595b7700`, requested three: a pending menu action surviving page teardown, `1e` saving the default expiration, and import focus that stayed owed. `633771492..58cf94715` fixed all three.
- Round 3, on `58cf94715`, accepted those fixes and raised the matrix scroll offset kept across the empty state. `910fd76bb` fixed it, and `1830b4ea0` fixed the round-3 nit.
- Round 4, on `1830b4ea0`, **accepted D05 for its declared scope** with no remaining blocker or should-fix. It ran 205 targeted tests in 26 files. Its acceptance does not cover exact-head CI.

CodeRabbit CLI 0.9.0 reviewed the committed diff `c7f87a53d..4595b7700` (78 files) and raised one minor finding, the L6 evidence wording, which `58cf94715` fixed. On `633771492..58cf94715` it raised one minor finding: that the Training upload still focused `Clear` in Chromium. The targeted rerun on `58cf94715` measured the `Preview` heading in Chromium 152, so no change was made.

**Loading status and Library gate check.** Tested revision `5dda10370` (`5dda10370d06b49808dd9fff059f88587feb41c7`, branch `refactor/stabilize-1603`, clean tree), worker 1, verify:app candidate digest `6ca29865` (`6ca2986561bcd6043e11ca2a04995cbb91e369768e1fab2cb62131a886a915c8`), origin `http://127.0.0.1:44759`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, native DevTools tools, 1280x800 light, Organization A. Actors `ictConfigurator` (M3, L7, person record), `ratingConfigure` (E5) and `calendarConfigure` (toolbar smoke). `6ee4c86f3`, `15b54deb5` and `7b5badc26` answer three hosted CodeRabbit findings on PR [#1620](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1620): `aria-busy` on four live-region loading statuses, `New Task` and `Import CSV` usable while the library loads, and a closing toolbar menu running a later item.

- **M3 PASS.** On Home, `server-source arm ict_tasks hold 1 120000`, then Critical Tasks. The loading element was a `DIV` with `role="status"`, name `Loading Critical Tasks` and `aria-live="polite"`. It had no `aria-busy`, and neither did any descendant or ancestor. `Export Excel`, `Import Completions` and `Record Completion` were disabled. After `server-source restore` the matrix rendered six body rows with `SPC Able, Synthetic` first.
- **L7 PASS.** The same hold, then `Task Library`. The loading status was a `SECTION` with `role="status"`, `aria-live="polite"` and the text `Loading ICT task library...`, with no `aria-busy` on it or any related element. `Import CSV` and `New Task` had `disabled` true and title `Critical Tasks are still loading.`. A native click on each failed as non-interactive, and no dialog or form opened. After release both were enabled with an empty title. `New Task` opened the form with focus on `ict-task-code` (`Task code`); `Cancel` closed it.
- **E5 PASS.** On Home, `server-source arm rating_scheme_entries hold 1 120000`, then Evals. The `SECTION` had `role="status"`, name `Loading evaluations`, `aria-live="polite"` and no `aria-busy`. After release the unseeded page read `No rating scheme entries yet`. With `seed rating-scheme` and the same hold, release rendered `7 of 9 shown` and the entry buttons.
- **Toolbar smoke PASS.** Calendar `Planning`, then `Assignment Planner`, navigated to `/calendar/assignments` (title `Assignment Planner`) and left no menu open.

**Loading status and Library gate UNRUN.**

- **Person record.** Its `Loading critical task record...` status was not reached. Holds on `ict_tasks` ordinals 1, 2 and 3 and on `personnel_ict_completions` ordinal 1 were tried on Able's record, by history Back from Home and by a hard URL load. Ordinals 1 and 2 and the completions read are awaited by the route's server loads. SvelteKit keeps the previous page while that navigation is pending, so the status never renders; the hard load blocks the document. Ordinal 3 never matched, and the record rendered at once. Source-mapped only: `6ee4c86f3` removes `aria-busy` from that status in the same way as the three observed live.
- **First choice in a closing menu.** A second choice during the roughly 110 ms close animation cannot be produced, because native calls are serialized. The component test `runs only the first item chosen while its menu is closing` covers it.

**Observations.** Not judged against a row. On a fresh actor page the first click on a sidebar link (`ICTLs`, then `Evals`) did nothing, with no navigation and no hold match; a second click navigated. The library loading `SECTION` has no accessible name of its own, only its text and a nested `svg` named `Loading`.

## Phase 10 D06 #1604

**Accepted and merged.** D06 [#1604](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1604), task `10-06`, merged as one PR, [#1618](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1618). It was squashed to `36cf8743` on 2026-10-11, and main's tree is identical to its accepted head `b21cbe31`. That head passed exact-head CI. CodeRabbit approved it after 6 of its 8 findings were fixed and 2 were dismissed against live observations. Most fixes were observed live. The rejected-fetch download error rests on a component test. The accordion id ordering rests on a component test and on rendering the same markup as an earlier live run. Independent acceptance round 4 accepted it after three holds. #1604 closed on merge. The baseline, final, follow-up, review-fix, acceptance-fix, toolbar, consumer-census and download runs below are its complete record.

**Unchanged-application baseline (verification preparation; no application change).** D06 [#1604](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1604): Onboarding and Sign-in Rosters on the canonical UI foundation. Recipe and baseline only. Tested revision `ebe6b4fd6` (main `5722aa9c5` plus the recipe commit; application source unchanged), candidate digest `bbbba417` (`bbbba41703f65aba521fe4529a3cea21f62b60ab582b65b8d2546d91f0a6e0c3`), worker 1, origin `http://127.0.0.1:37859`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82. Actors: `onboardingOperate`, `onboardingConfigure`, `onboardingView`, `onboardingEmpty`, `viewer`, `rosterManager`, `rosterViewer` and `trainingEmpty`, all in Organization A. Viewports: 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark (theme through the visible `Toggle theme`). The run was interrupted by an app restart after the 1280 roster steps; it continued on the same worker, candidate and origin after a passing `doctor`.

**Passes.**

- Board, operate: `Start Onboarding` creates one in-progress packet; Enter and Space open the sheet and focus moves in; `Mark Complete` and `Cancel` confirmations return focus on Escape; Enter and click on stages save; Escape and `Close` return focus to the card.
- Board roles: configure has `Mark Complete`, `Re-sync`, `Assign Template` (null template) then `Switch Template`, and `Cancel`; operate lacks the template actions; view has none and only `History` in the header.
- Board empty (`onboardingEmpty`) shows `No active onboardings` with its description; `viewer` is denied with a 403 page.
- Board at 390 dark: page width 390, the board scrolls inside its region (1200 in 366), the sheet fits (293 wide) and its actions wrap.
- History notes toggle: `aria-expanded`, resolving `aria-controls`, Enter, the app ring, note text and time. History empty shows `No onboarding history`.
- Templates: the listbox's `aria-selected` follows Enter selection; at 390 the list and detail swap with a back control; the delete confirmation names the template, Cancel returns focus to `Delete <name>` and confirm removes it and its steps; `onboardingView` and `onboardingOperate` are denied the direct route and have no `Templates` link.
- Roster create and `Generate & Print` save one row and a four-person PDF; native `Signed Scan` upload stores the fixture bytes; the file-name button downloads matching bytes; `Remove` clears the path and the object; `Delete Roster` names the roster and Cancel returns focus to `Delete` (1280 light and 390 dark), and confirm removes the row.
- Roster loading shows one spinner with `role="status"` `aria-label="Loading"`; `rosterViewer` has rows, toggle and `Re-print` without `New Roster`, `Delete`, the scan input or `Remove`; `trainingEmpty` shows `No sign-in rosters yet.` with `New Roster` and `0 total`.
- Roster modal at 390 dark: the dialog is 358 wide at x 16 with no overflow and page width 390; the filters stack; the `Signed Scan` field and the create form (rank chips wrap over three rows, the footer stacks) fit; `Generate & Print` is disabled until a title exists.

**Observations.** The board renders a `<main>` inside the shell's `<main>`; its columns are `h2` headings with a bare count and no list semantics; `Empty.Title` is not a heading. The view actor sees a `Record Training` link, where focus lands when the sheet opens. `viewer`'s denial is SvelteKit's 403 page, not the app-level `Access Restricted` card the recipe names. History's error is the root `500` page with `Back to Dashboard` and no Retry. The Templates listbox has no arrow-key movement (shared type-management pattern), and focus falls to `body` after a confirmed template delete. The roster dialog has two `Close` buttons and, when empty, two `New Roster` buttons; focus goes to the dialog container after Generate or scan `Remove` and to the `Title` filter after a cancelled create or a confirmed delete; the viewer has no signed-scan download although the scan endpoint admits Training read.

**Unreachable or UNRUN.** Board `Not configured` is UNRUN: no fixture Organization lacks template steps. Templates `Loading onboarding templates...` is UNREACHABLE: a full load with `personnel_onboardings` held renders the server list while the document loads, and no SPA link reaches Templates without the board's workspace. The roster `Uploading...` state was too brief to capture. Roster failed list, loading and empty at 390 dark are UNRUN (their 1280 results stand; the 390 layout is covered by the list capture). The issue's "Kanban drag and keyboard reordering" is source-mapped: the board has no drag or reorder (only template steps reorder, covered above).

**Review-fix run on `684c8ef8`.** CodeRabbit's review of PR [#1618](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1618) found two behavior gaps, both now repaired. First, History's failed load renders the Organization error page, which offered only `Back to Dashboard` against the recipe's "a way to retry". `dc235a74` adds `Try again` (reload) to that page for 5xx. 403 and 404 keep their single action, and every Organization route's server failure renders the same page. Second, a confirmed step delete left focus on `body`. `be126b9f` moves it to the surviving step's `Edit`, or to `Add Step` when no step survives. The run used digest `b0a7ae6e` (`b0a7ae6ed88449b439b9dd949757ef76a0a0703eacf737447746022008a461d4`), worker 1, origin `http://127.0.0.1:40097`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82.

**Download-failure run on `6674dbfe`.** CodeRabbit raised two findings on `d7744fe9`, and two commits fix them. In `dcd466ab`, a signed-scan link request that fails shows `Could not generate download link.` in the field. Before, a rejected fetch or a non-JSON reply left the user with nothing. In `6674dbfe`, the accordion content always keeps the id its trigger names, even if a caller passes its own. The run used worker 1, origin `http://127.0.0.1:44891` and Chromium 152.0.7977.82, as `rosterViewer` at 1280 light.

- **Failed link request.** PASS. A synthetic 503 on the Alpha roster's link request shows the error in the field.
- **Retry.** PASS. The error clears and the signed PDF opens: 3205 bytes, matching the stored file's SHA-256 (`e5f0d817…`).
- **Rejected fetch.** Covered by a component test that fails without the change. The control cannot produce a network rejection.
- **Accordion.** No live capture, because the read-only query used the wrong slot name. The change only moves `id` after the spread, and no caller passes an `id`. The rendered markup therefore equals what the acceptance-fix run verified, and the packet-list linkage test passes.

The archive is [`1604-download.tar.gz`](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/releases/download/evidence/1604-download.tar.gz), SHA-256 `1385b065bfd8e6ccb5777c9ef7b457d5f5096d58ec7ceb880b43a84b7002747e`, with 7 payload files plus `INVENTORY.sha256`. A fresh download matched every hash.

**Dispositions.** Every D06 baseline and later-run failure now passes on a candidate:

- **History accordion.** Repaired in the shared `ui/accordion` wrapper. Each trigger resolves to its own panel, collapsed and expanded.
- **Roster viewer and Training link.** The viewer's signed-scan download and the Training-gated `Record Training` pass live.
- **Board header at 390.** Passes on `55890842`.
- **Left to their shared owners.** D06 does not change these, and they stay with D09 [#1607](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1607):
  - Listbox arrow keys.
  - The duplicate dialog `Close`.
  - `Empty.Title` not being a heading.
  - The app shell's nested `main`.
- **Not configured state.** The disabled `Start Onboarding` explains itself only through the adjacent `Set up onboarding` text. It sits outside the Tab order, so a programmatic description would never be announced.

## Phase 10 D04 #1602

**Accepted and merged.** D04 [#1602](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1602), task `10-04`, merged as one PR, [#1615](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1615), squashed to `ef7f9baf` on 2026-10-10. Its exact head `a98a6053` passed all 7 CI checks, CodeRabbit approved, every review thread was resolved, and independent acceptance round 2 accepted it; #1602 closed on merge. The baseline, final run, acceptance rounds and checks below are its complete record.

**Unchanged-application baseline (verification preparation; no application change).** D04 [#1602](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1602): Dashboard and Training on the canonical UI foundation. Recipes, one seed and the unchanged-app baseline only. Tested revision `3cf35215e` (main, application source unchanged), candidate digest `c81a491d` (`c81a491d860635ca267d38b5e2c85b75b1a33312902f0e949a6b1fc435aacd5b`), worker 1, origin `http://127.0.0.1:39129`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82. Actors: `dashboardView`, `dashboardScoped`, `dashboardEmpty`, `dashboardNone`, `dashboardForeign`, `dashboardSwitch`, `trainingConfigure`, `trainingEmpty`, `viewer`, and for the in-page restricted attempt `rosterScoped`, `trainingScoped`, `trainingScopedConfig`, `trainingOperate`, `trainingView`, `trainingForeign`, `rosterViewer`. Failure steps used the synthetic 503 from `network arm-fail` and `server-source fail-hard`, which prove client recovery only. Final doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed.

| Item         | Result and limits                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Recipes      | New [Dashboard and Training states](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/training-form-states.md) recipe (raw controls, failed saves, field errors, screen states, color data, tables and print, in-page restricted attempt); [Dashboard](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/dashboard.md) step 10; `seed dashboard-overflow` (helper, test and `control.mjs` allowlist) so the three Overview toggles can be reached; the [component state reader](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/component-foundation.md) gains `pressed`, `sort` and dialog `busy`. The overflow seed's `expected` wording, the recipes and this section were written after the final doctor from this run and are not live-reverified. |
| Baseline run | Drove the selected steps at 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark where the table says so. Rows read back after `clean-seeds` and compared with the captures taken before the run (ignoring `updated_at`): Personnel A 3 and B 1, Training completions A 1 and B 0, Training types A 3 and B 1, Training views A 1 and B 1, all identical.
