**Acceptance follow-up.** Three commits follow the final run. `85eae6bc` restores two regressions that run observed: the group chip remove `Button` is 28x28 from `sm` and 40x40 below it (the cutover had made it 24x24), and the risk table column headers carry `scope="col"` again. `cc6db0e8` merges `main` at `55243788` (D03 Calendar settings and bulk); its only shared change outside Calendar adds a focus ring to `tabs-content.svelte`, which no Dashboard or Training file imports, and the generated inventory was regenerated rather than hand-merged. `e771f65c` returns focus to `Import` after a failed import request and clears the Views `Name is required.` error once a name is typed; one test each fails on the previous source.

Independent acceptance (gpt-6.1-sol) on `cc6db0e8` (digest `576e634d`, worker 2, origin `http://127.0.0.1:37993`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, actor `trainingConfigure`) found no application defect and measured at 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark:

- Remove Medic and Remove NCO are 28x28 at 1280 and 40x40 at 390.
- Enter on a chip moves focus to the next chip's remove, and on the last chip to `Search roles`, never `body`.
- Every risk column header has `scope="col"` and every type row header `scope="row"`.
- Enter then Space on `Name` sorts descending then ascending with `aria-sort` on the `th` and focus kept.

Final doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed. Archive [`1602-acceptance.tar.gz`](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/releases/download/evidence/1602-acceptance.tar.gz), SHA-256 `b173848d3b91c00982beca904f05765cd9bfb0a411aca20c7883b3542dad5799`, 69 payload files plus `INVENTORY.sha256`, re-downloaded and verified; checkout paths are replaced with `<checkout>`. Its own receipts also record a passing focused Vitest run (67 files, 641 tests) and `inventory:ui -- --check`.

The migration and deletion map:

- **Deleted.** `DashboardLoadError.svelte` and its `$features/dashboard` export, the Dashboard page's private `workspace-loading` style block, the Training layout's `renderHydrationError` derived, the Types and Views `throwRenderError` catch branches, and the unreachable in-page `Access Restricted` branch of `TrainingPageView`.
- **Added.** `WorkspaceLoadError.svelte` beside `WorkspaceAccessRestricted.svelte`, used by the Dashboard layout and page and the Training layout, and `DEFAULT_TRAINING_TYPE_COLOR` in the Training contracts.
- **Native elements kept.** The three print tables keep native `table` markup for print pagination and repeating headings, as the component catalog records.
- **Shared families.** `src/lib/components/ui`, `src/lib/components/patterns` and `src/app.css` have no D04 change.

The Svelte autofixer reports no issues for any of the 19 changed components. Its remaining suggestions concern `$effect` blocks that store a streamed promise's result or reset per-Person drafts; neither can be a `$derived`.

The carried-over UNRUN journeys keep their baseline observation because the D04 diff does not change their code:

- **Unchanged code.** Excel export, Sign-In Rosters, deletion-request approval, B isolation, Group-scoped writes, `trainingSwitch` timing, the starter-pack endpoint, and Dashboard sources, scope and navigation. Their policies, endpoints, load functions and export builder are untouched. The only change near them is the Export reason text.
- **Print cohorts.** The Dense, Short and Empty cohorts changed only by an `aria-label` on each print table; the layouts and pagination are unchanged.

**Acceptance round 2.** gpt-6.1-sol accepted PR head `a98a6053` with no blocking item. Exact-head CI passed: application CI, Select checks, CodeRabbit and both Vercel checks; Database contracts and `claude` were skipped. It reran the Svelte autofixer on the 19 changed components (0 issues) and confirmed the deletion map, the empty shared-family diff and the reuse justification against source. It drove `e771f65c` on `246a7ea7` (digest `419593e8`, worker 2, origin `http://127.0.0.1:43399`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, actor `trainingConfigure`) at 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark:

- A failed import request shows `Import failed`, focuses the enabled `Import`, and keeps all 15 cell values, the edited note and the selection.
- A blank Views Save shows `Name is required.` with its `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby`; typing a name removes the message, the invalid state and the description.

The only later commit, `a98a6053`, changes recipe wording, so those observations stand under `246a7ea7`. Final doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed, and no Training row or view was saved. Archive [`1602-acceptance-r2.tar.gz`](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/releases/download/evidence/1602-acceptance-r2.tar.gz), SHA-256 `b989326ea17745be8ae30dac1df33fb4cdc24ec3349eeeede1b7cfd5b158a6df`, 104 payload files plus `INVENTORY.sha256`, re-downloaded and verified; local paths are replaced with placeholders. The Export reason not tied by `aria-describedby` stays a Low, non-blocking observation.

**Accepted and merged.** D02 [#1600](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1600), task `10-02`, merged as one final PR, [#1612](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1612), squashed to `3cf35215` on 2026-10-10. Its exact head `93b25eb8` passed all 6 CI checks, CodeRabbit approved, and every review thread was resolved; #1600 closed on merge. <a id="round-4-focused-run"></a>**Round 4 focused run.** Commit `4b1fa903` holds Rank and archived-item confirmations open and busy until the request settles (a failure keeps the dialog with a destructive Alert and Confirm retries; success closes and moves focus), cancels overlapping rank submissions and locks Add pack while a rank request is pending, and disables a blank Manage Groups rename. Worker 1 drove those and the earlier 390-dark gaps on candidate `aea7f43a` (digest `6781b478`, origin `http://127.0.0.1:32783`), Chromium 152.0.7977.82, at 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark as `orgOwner`, `manager`, `personnelScoped`, `personnelEmpty`, `ictViewer` and `viewer`, using held and failed `network` helpers (a held request keeps the real server effect; a failed one returns a synthetic 503 with none). Every assertion in the per-step results files of the superseding selected run is PASS (the archive also keeps `archived-first-attempt/`, which has two failed assertions); the 1280 Add pack check has no results file and rests on the `ranks/30-addpack-*` readbacks. `archived-first-attempt/` is a superseded 1280 run whose two last-item heading assertions failed. It deleted the seed's metadata-only document first, and that DELETE returned a real server 500 (`observations/`), yet after reload the document row was gone and three items remained. That is a partial failure, not a no-effect failure and not a safe retry: `deletePersonnelDocument` (`src/features/personnel/personnelDocuments.server.ts:213`) deletes the row before `removeFile` (`src/features/personnel/artifactWrites.server.ts:333`) requires storage acknowledgement, and the seed's document has no stored object to acknowledge (inferred from the source; the storage call itself was not observed). By the same ordering a failed storage call for a stored file would leave the file behind after the row is gone. It is recorded as an out-of-scope server lifecycle observation and kept out of the D02 FAIL list: the UI cannot create a document without a stored file, so this state is reachable only through the metadata-only seed, and the ordering belongs to the Personnel artifact lifecycle, not to the settings forms D02 covers. The sequence was reordered and redone at both viewports (the real 500 keeps the dialog open, as designed). Not selected: Import, Soldier data sheet, Edit basic info, artifact forms and roster states at 1280 (reused from `6e42f909` and `9e44afe2`, not re-driven); `personnelScoped` roster at 390 not captured. Observations, none a FAIL: the Add rank form's reset after a successful add was repaired in `b4582d2c` (see [Rank form reset re-check](#rank-form-reset-recheck)); the Custom pack has no Remove pack control. Final doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed. Rows after cleanup match the before readbacks (Personnel A 3 and B 1, Groups A 2 and B 1, pins 0 and 0, ranks B 4); ranks A 27 for the Coast Guard pack (22) as before. Created People and custom ranks were removed through the visible controls.

<a id="rank-form-reset-recheck"></a>**Rank form reset re-check.** SvelteKit resets a form after a successful action. On `RanksSettingsPage.svelte` that left Add custom rank on its first options (Army, Officer, O-10) with a blank Tie-break order, so the next add failed, and could blank an open edit form's fields after a successful save. Commit `b4582d2c` takes the add defaults from `defaultValue` and `selected` options (Branch Custom, Category Custom, Pay grade Ungraded, State Active, Tie-break order `500`, Code and Label empty) and skips the reset for edit forms. Worker 1 drove only that behavior on candidate `b4582d2ca1a9dfa4a3b60eef9ab5939df4725774` (clean tree, branch head), verify:app candidate digest `cb2baff3` (`cb2baff373e21b53530966c8fbca148bab762bac4ece0088e09fed6061b8cf46`), origin `http://127.0.0.1:43095`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, as `orgOwner`, at 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark with native DevTools tools and the `network` helpers. The failure step is the synthetic 503 from `network arm-fail`, which proves client recovery only. Every assertion in the selected passes' per-step results files passed (25 per viewport). A first 1280 pass (tag `K7`) is kept as `attempt-1-driver-slip/`: its one failed assertion counted each row's `aria-label` twice in my script, not an application result; the script was fixed and the pass redone from scratch. Final doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed. Rank rows after cleanup match the before readbacks (Organization A 5, B 4); all nine ranks created (three in the first pass and three in each selected viewport pass) were removed through `Actions for CODE` then `Remove`, with the rows read back before and after. The [rank recipe](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/rank-settings.md) now states the post-success defaults and that the edit form keeps its values; that edit followed the run, so its wording is not live-reverified and changes the candidate digest, not the application.

<a id="datatable-row-detail-recheck"></a>**DataTable row-detail re-check.** The row-detail cell in `data-table-view.svelte` inherited `whitespace-nowrap` from `Table.Cell` and spanned the whole scrolled table. Commit `d485dae9` gives the cell `whitespace-normal` and wraps its content in `<div data-slot="data-table-detail" class="sticky left-2 w-[calc(100cqw-1rem)] min-w-0">`, and puts `@container/data-table` (`container-type: inline-size`) on the root in `data-table-root.svelte`. The shared-family change is the root's container type, which every DataTable consumer gets; the cell and wrapper change only affects tables that pass a row detail (Ranks and Availability Forecast). Worker 1 drove it on candidate `d485dae9e74a6ae948ecd530ca70f520be9d1970` (clean tree, branch head), verify:app candidate digest `ddc6f715` (`ddc6f715d1a6216f4e8b9411c7f461a51bb1619db4082b43fd2a0893f7692c16`), origin `http://127.0.0.1:45539`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, at 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark with native DevTools tools and the `network` and `rows` helpers. Actors: `orgOwner` (Ranks, Personnel, Settings), `calendarLane` (Calendar reports), `calendarOperate` (Calendar bulk), `ratingView` (Evals) and `ictViewer` (Critical Tasks reports), all in Organization A. Every assertion in the selected passes' results files passed: Ranks 63 at 1280 and 103 at 390, Availability Forecast 28 and 44. Earlier passes are kept as `attempt-1-driver-slip/` and `attempt-2-driver-slip/`: the 390 pass there failed five assertions because the driver required Save inside the viewport although it was inside the table and below the 844px fold, and two later passes stopped on a driver bug. Those were driver errors, not application results; the drivers were fixed and the passes redone from scratch. Final doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed. Rank rows after cleanup match the before readbacks (Organization A 5, B 4), and every rank created (seven, across all passes) was removed through `Actions for CODE`, Remove. The [rank recipe](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/rank-settings.md) and the [component-foundation recipe](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/component-foundation.md) now state the row-detail expectations; those edits followed the run, so their wording is not live-reverified and changes the candidate digest, not the application.

Reload kept each pin, and every run ended at 0 saved pins. The failure step is the synthetic 503 from `network arm-fail`, which proves client recovery only. All 159 assertions in `results-summary.json` are PASS. A first Pin run is kept as `attempt-1-driver-slip/`: my driver waited for a title that changes after pinning, so it timed out with Bravo pinned; that pin was removed through the visible control (0 rows) and the run was redone from scratch. Final doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed; the three Groups created for the remove step were removed through the visible Remove, and no Person was written. **Performance.** Measured on a `seed bulk-501` roster of 504 people with a warm cache and no throttling, five runs per step. The baseline and the final ran in separate stacks at a host load near 3, so gaps inside the run-to-run spread count as no difference. The bound is main-thread render of the 651-cell window, which is one long task per load.

DOM elements went from 1847 to 1844. The baseline notes list 3-Month on settled as 69; its recorded samples give 82. An end-of-run month load under swap pressure (median 230) is kept in the archive and superseded by a retake at baseline load.

**Fix round.** Grid controls no longer restyle `ui/button` internals per call site. `ui/button` gains `variant: 'cell'` (no radius, border or press offset, normal weight and leading, ghost hover colors, 2px inset `--ring` focus ring) and `size: 'none'` (no height, padding or gap), documented in the [component catalog](../component-catalog.md#uibutton). Date headers, the person row header, the group toggle and Pin, and long-range day numbers use them and pass only geometry and state colors. The fix archive's `class-diff.txt` shows all 48 existing variant and size outputs byte-identical, so no other consumer was re-captured; at the five call sites only inert utilities drop out and date headers and day numbers gain the inset ring (final drew an outer ring raised with `z-[2]`). On `5add36f8` the month and 3-Month probes at 1280 light and 390 dark match final with 0 differing pixels; the pinned band matches; all 72 Tab stops keep final's order with a visible ring. Escape and month selection by click, Enter or Space now return focus to the month title in month and 3-Month views; an outside click still leaves focus where the user clicked. `CalendarNavigation.test.ts` fails 3 of 3 without the fix. The cell classes on DateCell were measured and rejected: month load median first cell, paint and long task were 208, 233, 105 ms (218, 243, 111 in a first run at higher host load) against 196, 218, 99 native at matching load. Next/Prev did not change.

**Fix round 2.** Acceptance and CodeRabbit findings. Month day cells were unnamed buttons (82 in the fix run's snapshot). Render state now builds each cell's name once per row, `RANK Last, First, Weekday, Month D, YYYY`, and the title stays the description; long-range day numbers are named by full date. September 2026 has 90 named and 0 unnamed day cells at 1280 light and 390 dark, and role and name tests fail without the label. The month title and MonthYearPicker drop their hover, border, cursor and font overrides; the title keeps `[font:inherit]` to read as its heading and gains the canonical 1px transparent border (219x36 instead of 217x34). Headless Chromium reports `(hover: none)`, so hover was checked as the matching stylesheet rules (`hover:bg-muted`, `hover:text-foreground`), not rendered. LongRangeView headings sized 24px per day while 390 day cells were 28px; the baseline, final and fix probes all measured 28px cells, so the 120px heading and 364px roster shortfalls predate this PR and are derived, not captured on the baseline. One responsive `--long-range-col-width` now sizes cells, headings and roster: drift 0 at both widths, rosters 2384px and 2748px, the last day reachable. Two pre-existing defects were found live and fixed. Long-range rows used `overflow-hidden`, which made each row a scroll container, so names scrolled away (-319px at 600px of scroll); `overflow-clip` keeps them at the left edge at both widths. The workspace loading state sat flush against the card edge; it now starts 24px in at 1280 and 20px in at 390. Recipe step 7 (range failure and retry) and step 9 (held workspace) pass at 1280 light and 390 dark. The step 7 banner renders below the grid card at both widths; fix round 3 moves it. Assignment Types at 390 dark paints its badges at 43x20 without clipping; its Staff Duty row cuts off Edit and Delete at the card edge, a Settings row layout issue owned by the Settings PR. Month load on `dbc9260d` with 504 people: median first cell, paint and long task 199, 225, 99 ms (ranges 189-221, 214-244, 94-115) against 196, 218, 99 native, separate stacks at host load near 3, so no measurable difference.

**Fix round 3.** Acceptance found the range loading, failure and Retry feedback below the fold: the route rendered it after CalendarPageView, whose root fills the height, and the fix 2 captures had scrolled it into view. CalendarPageView now takes the range status and renders it as `ui/alert` between the toolbar and the grid, as Training shows workspace errors, with `role="status"`, `aria-live="polite"` and `Retry calendar load` in `Alert.Action`. The route test now finds the message and Retry inside the `main` landmark and checks Retry reloads the same quarter and clears the message; both route cases fail on the old placement. Baseline states on main, captured without scripted scrolling: the failure region starts at 800 in an 800px viewport at 1280 light and at 844 in an 844px viewport at 390 dark, and `Loading additional calendar dates...` starts at the same lines; a native click on Retry still recovers (503, then 200, region gone). The held workspace's `Loading Calendar data` is in view at 56 on both. On `074a633f` the failure region is at 136-174 (Retry 145-169) at 1280 light and 128-186 (Retry 137-161) at 390 dark, the held range load's loading region at 136-174 and 128-166, all fully in view; Retry recovers with 503 then 200, and the September facts and held workspace match step 9 before and after reload. With no feedback the September grid probe equals fix 2 at both combinations; the 1280 light screenshot is byte-identical and the 390 dark one differs by at most one channel level. While feedback shows, the grid starts below it.

**Performance.** Measured on a `seed bulk-501` roster of 504 people at 1280 light with five reloads. 507 roster rows render on both. First row 344-361 ms against 347-386 ms, one long task of 240-260 ms against 239-267 ms. Each load makes no `__data.json` or `calendar-range` request and one other fetch on both sides, with 112 assets against 113. Click to settled frame is 139, 59 and 335 ms for Select shown, Review and Back against 144, 66 and 325 ms (single samples). Settings sections still mount only the chosen panel.

**Repairs.** Settings sections are vertical `ui/tabs` that mount only the chosen panel. Status and assignment dialogs use `ui/field` with color `ui/input`, and the date-header choice is a `ui/radio-group` that reverts after a failed save (the old native radio kept the failed choice; the test fails on the old section). Row actions are named by their row, Delete uses the destructive Button, and rows wrap instead of clipping. Bulk Add, Remove and the Roster Picker move from route style blocks to `ui/card`, `ui/alert`, Badge and tokens; date and load errors use the destructive alert and the whole-entry warning uses the warning alert. Two repairs came from the final run. The tab panels took a Tab stop with no focus ring from bits-ui's default `tabindex` 0; the fix round gives every `ui/tabs` panel a visible focus outline. The partial-overlap alert stacked a call-site grid gap on the description's own paragraph margin; the gap is removed. Four new component tests fail on the old components.

**Deleted.** The BulkStatusAdd, BulkStatusRemove and RosterPicker `<style>` blocks with their 78 route variables, BulkStatusRemove's inline SVG and 4 hex colors, RosterPicker's unused `eyebrow`, `title` and `copy` props, the Settings `Section` type and chevron, the assignment section's null header path, its Label and CalendarStatusBadge compositions (replaced by `ui/field` labels and canonical Badge composition), and BulkStatusPage's dead `font-display` class.

**Shared consumers.** Until the fix round no `ui` or `patterns` family changed; its `ui/tabs` consumers are listed there. The Roster Picker's DataTable draws its own border inside the card, as in baseline.

**Dispositions.** `#c47f00`, `#4a4a47` and `#f8f7f4` stay as the color-input defaults because a color input accepts only `#rrggbb` and the chosen value is saved as the type's color; the reason is in source. Assignment badges keep `text-white` on the user-chosen color, as in MonthlyAssignmentPlanner and DailyAssignmentModal. No native control, `<style>` or `var(--…)` remains in the owned files. Inventory row `10-03` moves from 16 to 12 candidate files, 15 to 7 raw controls, 3 to 0 style blocks, 120 to 42 variables and 16 to 3 hex values; it scans 43 files with BulkStepHeading. The remaining candidates are PR 1's grid geometry and DateCell and PR 3's planner and reports.

**Checks on `341bebff`.** `npm run format`, `format:check`, `governance:check` (0 findings), `check` (7962 files, 0 errors, 0 warnings), `npx vitest run src/features/calendar src/routes/org/[orgId]/calendar` (72 files, 564 tests), `build`, `inventory:ui -- --check` and `git diff --check` all exit 0. `eslint .` without the gitignored `.verification` scripts reports 0 errors and the same 3 warnings; `npm run lint` also scans the archived PR 1 fix 2 probe, whose empty `catch` is its one error. The Svelte autofixer's only suggestion concerns BulkStatusAdd's existing default-status `$effect`, which is kept.

**Fix round.** The coordinator rebased the branch onto main `3cf35215` (#1612, which changed `ui/data-table` and `ui/dialog`). The previous `tabindex={-1}` on the three Calendar Settings panels overrode primitive behavior at the call site, and the missing ring affected every `ui/tabs` panel. `tabs-content` keeps the Bits tabpanel stop and adds `focus-visible:outline-solid focus-visible:outline-3 focus-visible:-outline-offset-3 focus-visible:outline-ring/50`, the Button's 3px `ring/50` color drawn inside the panel; the overrides and the test assertion pinning them are gone. The Button's outer `ring-3` was tried first and rejected: Calendar Settings' `overflow-hidden` parent sits flush on three sides and showed only the top edge. An inset ring, sketched on the Feedback panel, was hidden under the full-bleed empty-state card. The rejected captures are in the archive. [Provenance](../ui-foundation-provenance.md) records the adaptation, and two recipes now include the panel stop. Tab from the active tab reaches the panel with a visible outline on all four sides, and layout is unchanged, at these places: Calendar Settings, all three sections at 1280 light and Status Types at 390 dark (`calendarConfigure`); Admin Feedback and Access Requests at 1280 light (`platformReviewer`); the Critical Tasks import preview at 1280 light (`ictOperator`, cancelled with no rows written); and the Soldier Data Sheet at 390 light (`manager`), whose tabs render only below `lg`. SettingsActivityRail renders `Tabs.Root` with no list or panel on its only route, so the change cannot reach it. A component test would only restate bits-ui's `tabindex` or a class name, so the live observations are the proof.

**Post-rebase rechecks.** These ran on the same candidate at 1280 light and 390 dark and were compared with final-r2. Each Settings section's row Edit dialog closes on a clean Escape and returns focus to that row's Edit button. A whitespace name keeps Save Changes disabled. A dirty Escape keeps the dialog, and Cancel returns focus to the row's Edit button with nothing saved. RosterPicker on Add and Remove behaves as in final-r2: search, row and Select shown selection, Clear, Space on `Select Alpha group`, Enter on Collapse and Expand with the count kept, a three-state name sort and no select-column sort control. Review and Back keep the selection, and at 390 page scrollWidth is 390. The Add and Remove accessibility trees match final-r2 apart from the route announcer and final-r2's chosen inputs. The only behavior difference is the intended panel Tab stop in Calendar Settings. The archive was re-downloaded with `gh release download`, and its hash and every inventory entry match after re-extraction. A secret scan found no token or cookie. Doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed. Later commits `05d53d36` (test) and `69b75c39` (recipes) change no application source and were not driven again.

**Checks on `69b75c39`.** On the fix tree, `npm run format`, `format:check`, `governance:check` (0 findings), `check` (7965 files, 0 errors, 0 warnings), `build`, `inventory:ui` (no change) and `-- --check`, and `git diff --check` all exit 0. `npx vitest run src/features/calendar src/routes/org/[orgId]/calendar src/lib/components/ui/tabs` plus the IctCompletionBulkImport, SoldierDataSheetModal, admin Access Requests and Feedback page, and SettingsActivityRail tests passes 78 files and 605 tests after the pinned assertion was removed; the first run failed on that assertion alone. ESLint on tracked files reports 0 errors and the same 3 warnings, and `npm run lint` again fails only on the gitignored PR 1 probe. The Svelte autofixer reports no issues on `tabs-content` or CalendarSettingsPage. Prettier `--check` passes on the changed test and the four changed Markdown files (EVIDENCE, provenance and two recipes).

**Fix round 2.** Independent acceptance found two blocking defects, missing Remove proof, a brittle test and a stale comment. Each defect test is committed before its fix and fails on the code before it.

- **Cleared dates.** A missing date validated as invalid with no error, and Add and Remove summarized any range without an error, so a cleared date printed `Invalid Date`. Baseline's `bulk/1280-light-remove-dates-cleared-invalid-date.png` shows it, and final-r2 kept it. `validateDateRange` now names the missing date, and both summaries require a valid range. Live on Add and Remove at 1280 light and 390 dark, a cleared start or end shows `Choose a start date` or `Choose an end date` in the alert and summary, Review stays disabled and no `Invalid Date` appears.
- **Date header switch.** Clearing the old header and then failing to set the new one left none. The brief's set-first fix failed live with `Internal server error`. The `assignment_types_one_header_per_org` unique index rejects a second header, so the server already enforces one. The switch now clears first and sets the previous header back if the set fails. Tests drive the real store against a fake server that mirrors the index, covering success, a failed clear and a failed set. Live at 1280 light and 390 dark, switching to Group Duty and back persists after reload, and the rows return to their starting values. No documented `network` control admits assignment types, so the failure paths are component-test proof only.
- **Remove states.** At 1280 light and 390 dark, a `calendar-range` 503 shows the load error with the selection kept, and the retry returns 200. A status with no entries shows `No matching status entries were found`. For the 501-person removal, Add saves 500 rows, review shows `500 exact matches`, and Back leaves A and B byte-identical. A synthetic 503 on the one 500-ID DELETE keeps the review and shows `Failed to remove status entries`. The restored retry returns 200 and `Removed 500 status entries`. After reload no marked row remains, and B, unrelated A rows and the 504 People are unchanged.
- **Tests and comment.** The Roster Picker test checks for no sort control in the selection column and a named sort control in each data column, and it fails if the selection column becomes sortable. The Calendar Settings comment that called the panel not a Tab stop is removed. The **Deleted** entry no longer calls the replaced Label and CalendarStatusBadge imports unused.

**Header disposition.** The switch is still two writes. If restoring the previous header also fails, the Organization has no header type until someone chooses one; the index rules out two. A server-owned switch operation would make this atomic. It needs a migration, so it is left for a later server change.

**Checks on `572f0feb`.** `npm run format`, `format:check`, `governance:check` (0 findings), `check` (7966 files, 0 errors, 0 warnings), `npx vitest run src/features/calendar src/routes/org/[orgId]/calendar src/lib/components/ui/tabs` (74 files, 572 tests), `build`, `inventory:ui` (no change) and `-- --check`, and `git diff --check` all exit 0. ESLint on tracked files reports 0 errors and 3 warnings, all in files this round does not touch. The Svelte autofixer reports nothing on the changed components except BulkStatusAdd's existing default-status `$effect`. The archive was re-downloaded with `gh release download`, and its hash and every inventory entry match after re-extraction. A secret scan found no token or cookie. Doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed.

**Hosted review on `b40807af`.** CodeRabbit found that a second header choice made while a switch was pending could race the first. The radio group is now disabled until the whole clear, set and any restore finish, and a component test that holds the first write and picks another type fails without the guard. Live on digest `911bf7f2` (worker 1, origin `http://127.0.0.1:35767`, `calendarConfigure`, 1280 light), switching to Group Duty and back persists exactly one header type each time by `rows` readback, ending at the fixture's Staff Duty; the overlap itself has no live proof because no documented network control holds the assignment-type request. [`1601-settings-bulk-fix3.tar.gz`](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/releases/download/evidence/1601-settings-bulk-fix3.tar.gz), SHA-256 `eee98ff37ce4fce801dfa7534d154f36cb67fabbbc5690bbd265132da7f7c6da`, 7 payload files plus `INVENTORY.sha256`, re-extracted and re-downloaded. The catalogs recipe's keyboard step now tabs until the named Edit action has focus.

In scope are MonthlyAssignmentPlanner, AssignmentPlannerPage, the five Calendar reports and the Reports route. AssignmentPlannerPage, CalendarReports and the four other reports had no candidates and are unchanged.

All four archives were re-downloaded with `gh release download` and match the local hashes, and each inventory was verified by re-extraction. A secret scan of each found no credential, token or cookie. Doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed after each run, with Organization A's four seeded September 15 assignment rows and Organization B's one row unchanged; the fix run wrote nothing and read back only Organization A's four rows. Each archive's `notes.md` has the per-step detail.

**Performance.** Measured on a `seed bulk-501` roster of 504 people at 1280 light, five runs per step, warm cache, no throttling, host load about 3.5 for the baseline and 2 for the final. Planner first slot paint is 201 ms median (188-242) against 204 ms (184-245), one long task per load lasting 86-102 ms against 83-104 ms (each archived long-task sample is a two-value pair whose second value is the duration; the first value is its start offset), 1188 DOM elements on both and one API request per load. Opening a slot with 504 options takes 138 ms median against 145 ms. Status days for 2026 settles in 145 ms against 147 ms with 3868 elements against 3869; the merged route wrapper removes one. Group availability for one month settles in 49 ms on both, about three frames, with 454 elements against 415. DataTable wraps each of the 31 unsortable date headers in a span and adds two sort icons. The seed adds no groups, so that report measures two rows. All gaps fall inside the run-to-run spread, so the cutover shows no measured cost.

**Repairs.**

- **Planner table.** MonthlyAssignmentPlanner renders its month through `ui/table` with the name `MONTH YEAR assignments` and column scopes. Weekend and today rows keep their token tints; header type badges keep `text-white` on the user-chosen color, as in PR 2.
- **Planner controls.** The Quick Fill day select is named `Quick Fill days`. The month header no longer wraps: the controls do not shrink and the heading truncates at `text-base` below `sm`. A component test checks the table name, headers, row count and select name, and fails on the unchanged component.
- **Group availability.** The table is `ReportWorkspace.Table` with a feature-owned `useDataTable` state sorted by lowest availability, the sibling reports' structure. Sorting, `aria-sort` and direction icons come from the shared sortable header, and a `row` snippet paints day cells with token tones. A component test checks the initial order, both sort controls in both directions and their `aria-sort`; it passes on the old component too, so it guards the migration rather than proving a repair.
- **Report column order.** Status days, Away days and Assignment load listed their type columns in the order entries arrived from the provider, so the baseline showed Status days as Leave, Retiring Status, Training and Assignment load as Archived, Staff, Retiring, and the final launch happened to show the catalog order. `computeStatusDays` and `computePersonnelTempo` now take the status type catalog, and all three return their active type IDs in catalog order (`sort_order` from the catalog query). The visible tables and the workbook exports already follow that list. Three unit tests feed entries in reverse catalog order and fail on the old functions. In the fix run the report API delivered assignments as Archived, Staff, Retiring, and the table and the `Assignment Coverage` sheet read Staff, Retiring, Archived. Status days read Leave, Training, Retiring Status and Away days Leave, Training, the Calendar Settings order, at 1280 light and 390 dark, on the first run and after a reload, and in the `Status Days` and `Personnel Tempo` sheets. That launch delivered availability already in catalog order, so the unit tests carry the reversed status case. Types created in Settings get `sort_order` 0, so ties fall back to the catalog query's second sort key, `id`, so their order is stable across loads and shared by Settings and the reports.
- **Reports title.** The route sets `Calendar Reports - Troop to Task` like Training Reports and no longer nests a `main` inside the layout's. A route test checks the title and the absent landmark, and fails on the unchanged route.

**Deleted.** Group availability's two raw sort buttons, its `groupSort` state and toggles, the `color-mix(var(--success|--warning|--destructive))` cell skins and the empty-groups state. That state was unreachable: Run needs at least one group and every selected group yields a row; the table keeps an empty message. The planner's raw `table` and overflow wrapper, and the Reports route's nested `main`.

**Shared consumers.** No `ui` or `patterns` family changed. Group availability now consumes `ui/data-table` and `ui/table` through `patterns/report-workspace` like the other four reports, and the planner consumes `ui/table` directly.

**Duty remnants.** No Duty Roster UI remains. A search of `src` for duty finds the retained Staff Duty assignment-type seed and settings copy, the Personnel duty-role attribute (`By Duty Role`, `MOS / Duty Role`), and dated changelog entries that record the removal.

**Issue acceptance.** `10-03` is complete once this final PR passes exact-head CI and review and merges.

- **Migrated consumers.** PR 1 moved Calendar, CalendarHeader, CalendarRow, GroupHeader, LongRangeView, MonthYearPicker, CalendarNavigation, CalendarPageView, `date-cell/*` and `calendar-status-badge/*` onto `ui/button` (with the central `cell` variant), alerts and tokens. PR 2 moved CalendarSettingsPage and its three sections onto `ui/tabs`, `ui/field`, `ui/card` and `ui/alert`, and BulkStatusPage, BulkStatusAdd, BulkStatusRemove and RosterPicker onto `ui/field`, `ui/alert` and `ui/data-table`. PR 3 moved MonthlyAssignmentPlanner onto `ui/table` and GroupReadiness onto `ReportWorkspace.Table`, and named the Reports page.
- **Deleted paths.** TodayBreakdown and its test; the Calendar, GroupHeader, BulkStatusAdd, BulkStatusRemove and RosterPicker `<style>` blocks with their route variables; BulkStatusRemove's inline SVG and hex colors; RosterPicker's unused props; the Settings `Section` type; and the PR 3 deletions above.
- **Retained interactions.** Month and long-range grids with virtualized cells, date and person controls, pinned bands and picker focus return; Settings lifecycle, dialogs and the date-header choice; bulk Add, Remove and Import with partial overlap and retries; planner slots, Quick Fill, Clear all and month navigation; all five reports with filters, states and exports; Calendar access, scoped and denied states. Each was driven at 1280 light and 390 dark in its PR's runs.
- **Shared-consumer review.** The `ui/button` `cell` variant serves the Calendar date headers, person row headers, group bands and long-range day numbers, all live-checked in PR 1. CalendarStatusBadge consumers (StatusLegend, AvailabilityModal, the three bulk flows, both settings sections and the dashboard OverviewTab) were each observed in PR 1 and PR 2. PR 2's fix round observed the `ui/tabs` panel outline at each reachable consumer: Calendar Settings, Admin Feedback and Access Requests, the Critical Tasks import preview and the Soldier Data Sheet. SettingsActivityRail renders no panel.
- **Kept native, variable and hex.** The month DateCell stays a native button because `ui/button` cells cost load and navigation time (PR 1); the reason is in source. `--calendar-cell-width`, `--calendar-personnel-column-width`, `--dates-count` and the `--long-range-*` sizes stay as CSS variables because virtualization reads them as layout state (PR 1). `#c47f00`, `#4a4a47` and `#f8f7f4` stay as color-input defaults because a color input accepts only `#rrggbb` (PR 2). `BulkStatusRemoveLoadRangeStub` and `DatePickerInputStub` keep a native button and input because they are test fixtures standing in for real components. The inventory row reads 43 files, 10 with candidates, 3 raw controls, 0 style blocks, 39 variables and 3 hex values, and each is one of these.
- **Named observations.** The Reports title is repaired and verified above. The report column order, recorded after the final run, is repaired and verified in the fix run ([`1601-planner-reports-fix.tar.gz`](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/releases/download/evidence/1601-planner-reports-fix.tar.gz), SHA-256 `822285a54d79ba7c922fa68bbbf318189d8ff0420527d29f4fb8186e2a93fc29`). The CalendarPageView in-page Access Restricted margin was repaired in PR 1 by giving its full-width `Empty.Root` `w-auto`, so the margins no longer push the card past its column. The branch is unreachable for current roles, so the repair is source-mapped, and the workspace card covers the reachable denial. The PR 2 header switch remains two writes, as recorded in its **Header disposition**, because an atomic switch needs a migration.

**Checks on `7bba70d2`.** `governance:check` (0 module-boundary findings), `check` (7968 files, 0 errors, 0 warnings), `test` (758 files, 7467 tests), `build` and `inventory:ui -- --check` exit 0. ESLint over the tracked files reports 0 errors and 3 warnings, all pre-existing in `CalendarPageContext.svelte.ts` and the onboarding store; `npm run lint` also reads ignored verification scripts and fails only on those. On the records commit, `npm run format`, `format:check`, explicit-path `npm exec prettier -- --ignore-path /dev/null --check` on the changed Markdown, `node scripts/ci/changes.mjs --documents`, a link and anchor check and `git diff --check` exit 0. The new tests fail on the unchanged planner and route as described under **Repairs**.

**Checks on `fafbb274`.** `governance:check` (0 module-boundary findings), `check` (7968 files, 0 errors, 0 warnings), `npx vitest run src/features/calendar src/routes/org/[orgId]/calendar` (75 files, 577 tests), `build` and `inventory:ui -- --check` exit 0. ESLint over the tracked files reports 0 errors and the same 3 warnings. The three column order tests fail on `7cf73e16` with the arrival order (`['medical', 'school', 'leave']`, `['medical', 'leave']`, `['guard', 'staff-duty']`) and pass on the fix. On the records commit, `npm run format`, `format:check`, explicit-path prettier on the changed Markdown, `node scripts/ci/changes.mjs --documents` and `git diff --check` exit 0.

**Rebase and final records.** The branch was rebased onto main `ef7f9baf` (D04 #1615), which shares only the verification helper `control.mjs`, the verify skill and fixture helpers with this PR and no `ui`, `patterns` or Calendar file; the inventory was regenerated. The Svelte autofixer reports no issues for `MonthlyAssignmentPlanner`, `GroupReadiness`, `PersonnelTempo`, `StatusDaysSummary` and the Calendar Reports route. The complete required sequence runs as exact-head CI on the published PR; locally `npm run lint` fails only on gitignored `.verification` probe scripts, and ESLint on tracked files reports 0 errors.

Branch `stabilization/1599-ui-cutover` carries the cutover; its final run, repairs and checks follow the baseline material, which stays as recorded. The unchanged-application baseline ran on revision `5ab07896` (application source identical to main `82a07be1`; only verification helpers, recipes and docs differ), verify:app candidate digest `abf6bb5a`, worker 1, origin `http://127.0.0.1:36193`, Chromium 152.0.7977.82, synthetic Organizations A/B. The migration PR repeats these steps on its candidate. UNRUN marks a combination with no capture; unreachable names the missing route.

**Failing baseline observations.** (1) Owned by D01: at 390px after Send Feedback inside the drawer, the drawer stays open and the success toast paints above it (toaster `z-index: 999999999`, sheet and overlay `z-index: 50`) but sits behind it for input. The modal drawer sets `pointer-events: none` on `body`, `elementFromPoint` at the toast center returns the drawer's Account menu trigger, a native Close toast click leaves `data-removed="false"`, and Alt+T leaves focus in the drawer. At 1280px with no drawer the same close works. (2) The 390px drawer is portaled outside the sidebar wrapper and renders in the public body font (Source Serif 4) while the desktop sidebar, header and main use the app sans stack. (3) The breadcrumb clips the current crumb at 390px without an ellipsis. (4) `/suspended` sets no page title. (5) Reset Password shows no mismatch message (submit only disables), while Account Security lists "Passwords match". (6) After a completed password change, a fresh session's Account Security shows "This attempt expired or belongs to another session" until Start new password update; this is identity attempt policy outside D01 presentation and needs an owner. (7) The MFA challenge error alert is not associated with its field. Items 2–5 and 7 need D01 disposition against the foundation contract.

**Run constraint.** The app hook allows 10 `/auth/*` POSTs per 15 minutes per client address across all owned contexts; one recovery Continue hit the 429 before its action ran and succeeded after the window reset. The identity recipe now budgets for it.

**Final candidate run.** Worker 1 drove the baseline's selected steps (shell steps 13–14, sign-in step 7, identity steps 1–12 and the Logout local-only state) on candidate `d0062cab` (digest `f1708c7d`, origin `http://127.0.0.1:33301`), Chromium 152.0.7977.82, at 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark, plus the extra observations below. Actors: `manager`, `ictViewer`, `calendarEmpty`, `platformReviewer`, `identityMfa`, `identityRecovery`, `identitySecurity`, `identitySuspended`, invite and setup-link accounts and public contexts. It found two D01 defects. Both are repaired in `7fa71569` and `6650ac29` with failing-first component tests, and the affected cases plus one adjacent consumer each were rerun on `6650ac29` (digest `ec376d12`, origin `http://127.0.0.1:45657`). Every other result is reused from `d0062cab`, whose application differs from `6650ac29` only in those two repairs. [`1599-final.tar.gz`](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/releases/download/evidence/1599-final.tar.gz), SHA-256 `b84dc96b894a153cca1183408642eee8bc95dd1f07b444efc8f03da44357328f`, 296 payload files plus `INVENTORY.sha256`. Re-downloaded into fresh scratch: archive hash, every inventory hash and the exact file set verified. Link tokens and the MFA QR data URL are redacted, and the QR panel was never screenshotted. `clean-seeds` and `doctor` on `d0062cab` were refused as `STALE_CANDIDATE` once the repair commits existed; `verify:app restart` then disposed that provider and its seeds. On `6650ac29`, `clean-seeds`, final doctor and owned cleanup passed.

**Field errors and shared messages.** Server and API failures that name one field now render as `Field.Error` on that field: MFA verify and challenge 401s, password mismatch on Reset Password, Accept Invite, Account Setup and Account Security, the recovery-request 400 on Forgot and Renew Invite, and Account Security's incorrect current password, invalid or unchanged address, missing email-change password, rejected enrollment code and rejected disable code. Transport, rate-limit, provider and MFA-status failures stay form alerts. Account Security picks the field by message, so each such message is one exported constant in `src/features/identity/contracts.ts`, imported by the emitting handler and the page: `INVALID_EMAIL_MESSAGE` (`account/security/+page.server.ts`, `auth/forgot-password/+page.server.ts`, `auth/renew-invite/+page.server.ts`), `SAME_EMAIL_MESSAGE` and `EMAIL_CHANGE_PASSWORD_REQUIRED_MESSAGE` (`account/security/+page.server.ts`), `INCORRECT_CURRENT_PASSWORD_MESSAGE` (`account/security/+page.server.ts`, `api/auth/update-password/+server.ts`), `MFA_ENROLLMENT_CODE_REJECTED_MESSAGE` (`api/auth/mfa/enroll/verify/+server.ts`) and `MFA_DISABLE_CODE_REJECTED_MESSAGE` (`api/auth/mfa/disable/+server.ts`), all consumed by `account/security/+page.svelte`. The auth pages choose the field by status code. Wording is unchanged. The live run exercised the incorrect-password, unchanged-address, enrollment-code and disable-code constants end to end.

**Toast over modal families.** `ui/dialog`'s only production consumer is `AppDialog` and `ui/alert-dialog`'s only production consumer is `ConfirmDialog`. On Personnel (manager, 1280 dark) a synthetic 503 on the Organization A Personnel PUT (`network arm-fail`, nothing written) raised `Failed to update personnel` over Edit Personnel; then Archive opened `ConfirmDialog` over it; then the row Archive opened a top-level `ConfirmDialog` with a paused success toast. Each time `body` had `pointer-events: none`, the toast centre and Close toast were the hit target, a native click removed the toast and every open dialog stayed open with its draft. With a modal open, Alt+T leaves focus inside the modal's focus scope, as in the baseline. The toast is still announced through the polite `Notifications alt+T` region and auto-dismisses. Without a modal, Alt+T focuses the toaster.

**Migrated consumers.** `AppShellHeader` (frame offset through `below-banner`, breadcrumb truncation), `AuthShell` (body font through `font-body`, override hooks removed), `PasswordMfaChallenge`, root `+layout`, root and Organization `+error` (`ui/empty`), `WorkspaceAccessRestricted` (`ui/empty`), `/suspended` (`ui/card`), `/account/profile` (`NativeSelect`), `/account/security`, and `auth/accept-invite`, `forgot-password`, `mfa-verify`, `renew-invite`, `reset-password` and `setup-account`. The sign-out controls on Accept Invite, MFA verify and Reset Password are `Button variant="link"`. `ui/sonner` keeps toasts clickable above modal layers. The document default font is `var(--font-sans)` and Auth Shell keeps `font-body`, so portaled overlays inherit the app sans.

**Deleted paths and styles.** `<style>` blocks in `AuthShell`, root `+layout`, root `+error`, Organization `+error` and `/suspended`; the `--public-brand-*` overrides and `.auth-brand`; the inline `--banner-height` style in `AppShellHeader`; the raw sign-out `<button>`s and the profile `<select>`; Account Setup's separate mismatch notice. `src/routes/org/[orgId]/PreferenceProbe.svelte` moved to `src/features/app-shell/test-support/`. The regenerated inventory shows packet `10-01` with 33 files and only `PreferenceProbe`'s three native controls as candidates: no `<style>` block, `var(--…)` or hex literal.

**Native exceptions.** Account Profile's rank list stays `NativeSelect` because its form posts natively and works before hydration. `PreferenceProbe` keeps a native button pair and checkbox because it is a test fixture that asserts a native checkbox.

**Shared-family consumer review.** `ui/sonner` (changed): header and drawer feedback, Personnel's `AppDialog` and `ConfirmDialog` consumers, and the Toaster component test over a dialog. `ui/empty` (newly consumed here): Calendar's `No personnel added yet` state (`calendarEmpty`) stays inside its column at 1280 and 390 in the app sans; `/admin` renders in sans. `BannerStack` with `below-banner`: with a seeded announcement on Calendar, `--banner-height` is 49px at 1280 and 97px at 390, and the frame's margin-top, height and max-height follow it with no document overflow. Without a banner the variable is 0px and the frame fills 800px. Font default: `/dashboard`, the Organization home, Personnel, Calendar, `/account/profile`, `/admin`, `/org/new`, both error boundaries and `/suspended` render visible text in the app sans; `/`, `/about` and `/auth/*` keep only Barlow Condensed, IBM Plex Mono and Source Serif 4 text.

**Dispositions.** No `BrandWordmark` variant: AuthShell's `--public-brand-*` overrides equalled the defaults, so they were deleted; the public variant moves to D08 [#1606](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1606) with its only consumer, `PublicSiteNav`. `PreferenceProbe` keeps native controls (above). The stale password-attempt message after a completed password change (baseline observation 6) is server policy in `public.password_change_attempt` ([`20260918000200_allow_completed_password_attempt_restart.sql`](../../supabase/migrations/20260918000200_allow_completed_password_attempt_restart.sql)): the `session_id <> p_session` expired branch is evaluated before the completed state. It is outside D01's UI scope and is recorded for Phase 11 (security and reliability). This run saw it again on the 390 recovery page; the fresh Account Security session after the password change did not show it.

**Layering run.** The final run's two app-frame overlay observations were D01 app-shell defects. Both are repaired in `e30df54a`. (1) The Leave Organization dialog opened from the 390 drawer behind the drawer, and its Cancel could not be clicked. A resize from 1280 to 390 is enough, without navigation or a reload. Bits portals join `<body>` when the component mounts, not when it opens. `AppShellHeader` always mounts the Leave Organization `AppDialog`, while `ui/sidebar` mounts its `Sheet` only at mobile width. After the resize the drawer's nodes therefore followed the dialog's (`<body>` child nodes 64/79 after 31/46). Both used `z-50`, so the drawer painted on top and owned the hit test at Cancel's centre. After a reload at 390 the order was reversed and the dialog worked. (2) `BannerStack`'s `z-[90]` drew the announcement over every dialog overlay and the 390 drawer. At 390 the banner covered the drawer's Switch organization row and the top edge of What's New. The fix names the fixed layers in `src/app.css`, documented in the [component catalog](../component-catalog.md). From lowest to highest they are the desktop sidebar (`z-10`), `z-banner` (30), `z-sheet` (40) for `ui/sheet` and its overlay, then dialogs, alert dialogs and floating content at shadcn's `z-50`, then the Toaster. No component test was added, because the defect is CSS paint order and hit-testing, which happy-dom does not evaluate and [Testing strategy](../architecture/TESTING.md) leaves to visual evidence. Worker 1 reproduced both defects on `37059676` (candidate digest `ec376d12`, origin `http://127.0.0.1:35631`) and reran on the fix (digest `66cdfe96`, origin `http://127.0.0.1:41563`; built from the working tree committed unchanged as `e30df54a`), Chromium 152.0.7977.82, `manager`, at 390x844 dark and 1280x800 light. The fix run passed every case:

- At 390 dark, after client navigation at 1280 and a resize, Leave Organization from the drawer is the top, interactive layer even with the failing DOM order. A native Cancel click closes it, with focus back on the drawer's Account menu and the drawer still open.
- Drawer What's New and Send Feedback (including its Category select), and the drawer's Switch organization and Account menus, are hit targets above the drawer.
- The toast after drawer Send Feedback is the hit target over the drawer. It auto-dismissed before a native Close click.
- With a seeded announcement, the 390 drawer, What's New and Leave Organization draw above the banner, and the banner's Dismiss is reachable with no modal open.
- At 1280 light the banner stays above the desktop sidebar with the 49px `below-banner` offset and no document overflow. What's New's overlay covers the banner.
- A synthetic 503 toast over Edit Personnel is dismissed by a native Close toast click while the dialog stays open.

The other `ui/sheet` consumers have their own live run, recorded in the Sheet consumer run below, in place of the earlier source-only reading. Final doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed. [`1599-layering.tar.gz`](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/releases/download/evidence/1599-layering.tar.gz), SHA-256 `78377f3b68fed86c2c633b11423c57c0fd5d386927ea34641393111c761bafd6`, 61 payload files plus `INVENTORY.sha256`. Re-downloaded with `gh release download` into fresh scratch: archive hash, every inventory hash and the exact file set verified. No captures needed redaction. The [shell recipe](../../.agents/skills/verify-trooptotask/features/shell-preferences.md)'s step 15 was written from this run after cleanup and records its driven cases. It changes the candidate digest but not the application.

**Sheet consumer run.** Worker 1 drove the remaining `ui/sheet` consumers on `04f4683b` (candidate digest `acbf259b`, origin `http://127.0.0.1:42587`), Chromium 152.0.7977.82, at 1280x800 light and 390x844 dark, with a seeded announcement banner. Settings Members' Manage Access (`orgOwner`) and Onboarding's detail sheet (`onboardingConfigure`, a legacy packet and a temporary templated packet) draw above the page, the desktop sidebar and the banner: sheet and overlay `z-index` 40, banner 30, and `elementFromPoint` returns the overlay over each. Every select, alert dialog, Assign Template and Switch Template dialog opened from inside them is `z-50` and the hit target. Native Cancel closes each dialog with focus back on its opener, and Escape inside a select closes only the select. The sheet's Close control and Escape return focus to the row's `Manage role & access` button or the packet card. The Remove Member dialog opens from the row behind the sheet, so it is not reachable from inside it. It was observed standalone at 390 and is `z-50` with a working Cancel. The admin mobile drawer (`platformReviewer`) is `z-40` over its overlay, which is the hit target at the page edge. It offers no floating layer or Close control, and Escape returns focus to Toggle Sidebar. At 1280 admin renders no sheet, and it has no announcement area. No row was saved. The temporary Able packet was cancelled through the visible confirm and remains `cancelled` in the discarded disposable provider. The Members select options were hit-tested, not picked. Final doctor, `clean-seeds` and owned cleanup passed. [`1599-sheets.tar.gz`](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/releases/download/evidence/1599-sheets.tar.gz), SHA-256 `16cdd0bb0606b954a0d2509762fc35028819b48b902903993d334a7bccac1b32`, 114 payload files plus `INVENTORY.sha256`. Its own `notes.md` has the per-case table.

**Observations not caused by this candidate.** Each is outside the D01 diff and inferred from the source. None was rerun on unchanged main. (1) `CalendarPageView` and `TrainingPageView` add margins to a full-width `Empty.Root` in their in-page Access Restricted branches, so the card overflows its column. This is the pattern repaired in `WorkspaceAccessRestricted`. Calendar belongs to D03 [#1601](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1601) and Training to D04 [#1602](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1602). (2) MFA and Reset Password render `sign out .` with a space before the period, as in the baseline.

**Checks on `e30df54a`.** The full suite ran on `e30df54a` while this record was being written. The commit that records the layering run adds only documentation, and `format:check`, `governance:check` and `git diff --check` were rerun on its final text. With `PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:9` and `PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=placeholder`, `npm run format` and `format:check` pass. `npm run lint` passes with 0 errors and the 3 existing `svelte/prefer-svelte-reactivity` warnings in Calendar and Onboarding files outside this branch. `governance:check` reports 0 findings. `npm run check` reports 0 errors and 0 warnings over 7,961 files. `npm run test -- --maxWorkers=6` passes 752 files and 7,375 tests. `npm run build` passes with the existing optional `@opentelemetry/api` resolution warning and Rolldown chunking notices in modules this branch does not touch. `inventory:ui -- --check` and `git diff --check` pass. The Svelte autofixer reports no issues on `BannerStack`, `sheet-content` and `sheet-overlay`. Its suggestions concern unchanged `bind:this` and ResizeObserver lines. On `6650ac29` it reported no issues on the changed components except `goto()`/`href` without `resolve()` on unchanged lines in Account Security, MFA verify, Accept Invite, Reset Password and Forgot Password, identical on main. Exact-head CI is pending.

**Phase 11-02 pointer:** The transactional Calendar assignment prerequisite for D06 is already delivered. [#1408](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1408) merged as `1b9b7244` with migration `20260928000100`, and D06 #1410 routes every replacement through it. Phase 11 planners should start from its [pinned receipt](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/8072a9efbfb3438da415ac6681eeee8666b44860/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L642-L656).

**Preserved security and schema proof:** All isolated SQL ran in seed-disabled disposable projects with Supabase CLI 2.101.0 and PostgreSQL 17.6, followed by exact-project disposal. Disposable containers alone disabled `supautils` hint decoration; grants, RLS and denial assertions were unchanged. No agent touched an existing local or remote database. Every hosted application below is owner-reported, without agent ledger readback.

- **D02 setup claims:** `20260923000100` serializes same-email setup claims in a service-role RPC, returns `claim-in-progress` before Auth and revokes direct anon/authenticated setup-link writes. `20260923000200` adds the audited service-role staff release. Fresh 140-migration replay passed 36 claim-gate and 27 release assertions with dblink interleavings. A populated 138-to-140 upgrade passed 16/16 on exact rows, grants and held claims. Historical duplicate claims stay held and fail closed. The #1381 recovery passed 141 fresh migrations and 19 populated-upgrade assertions in its [accepted record](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/5fa75cf772d5a929408e97443b9cb005b4a71023/docs/stabilization/phase-08/1381-account-setup-recovery.md).
- **D03 lane and Group scope:** Migrations `20260926000200` to `20260926001200` repair Onboarding, Training, Critical Tasks, Calendar, Personnel and Rating Scheme lane and Group scope, task-reference integrity, counseling files, Soldier Data Sheet and configure-only sensitive reads. `20260926001200` binds pending counseling request status and row deletion atomically (17 transaction assertions). The Critical Tasks composite task-reference constraint remains `NOT VALID` for historical mismatches. Fresh 152-migration replay and populated 139-plus-13 upgrade passed with exact 26-row preservation.
- **D04 Calendar catalog:** `20260926001300` limits configuration and special-day writes to Organization configure and binds availability status references through a same-Organization composite FK `NOT VALID`. The global status-ID cascade becomes `NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED NOT VALID`, so cross-Organization history blocks deletion. Fresh replay passed 153 migrations / 41 suites / 986 assertions, and the populated upgrade passed 10 contracts / 125 assertions. Robert reports this migration live.
- **D06 assignment replacement:** `20260928000100` adds invoker-rights `replace_daily_assignment_slots`. It takes per-Organization/date advisory locks, checks Calendar capability and targets, and reads RLS-hidden occupancy through unexposed `calendar_private`. Fresh replay passed 154 migrations / 40 suites / 999 assertions, including replacement 27/27 and concurrency 11/11. The focused predecessor upgrade kept snapshot SHA-256 `de50837ffefc360ce7eb22ceac6922f889a3fb8854c252ab4f3ca72d9fd60392`. Direct table writers do not take the advisory lock.
- **D08 Training catalog:** `20260929000200` requires Training configure with Organization scope for catalog writes and replaces globally destructive type-reference actions with same-Organization actions and deferred mismatch protection. Fresh replay passed 156 migrations / 42 suites, including `training_catalog_rls.sql` 46/46. The populated upgrade passed 31/31 and retained 12 historical rows.
- **D10 Critical Tasks configuration:** `20260929000100` aligns library writes with configure scope. Fresh 155-migration replay and populated 140-migration upgrade passed. Robert reported it live before #1413 merged.
- **D12 Personnel:** `20260929000300` enforces Personnel Group reference integrity and `20261001000100` adds the service-role-only `delete_expired_personnel` retention RPC. The pinned D12 records carry their proof.
- **D13 artifacts:** At `1d940b66`, a fresh 159-migration replay passed 46 suites / 1,213 assertions, including sensitive reads 43, counseling file scope 40, artifact Group scope 27, note traversal 32 and counseling deletion atomicity 17. `personnel_document_cap_concurrency.sql` proves the two-session document cap with 12 fresh assertions. Retention deletes in bounded batches of at most 1,000 Storage paths. D13 adds no migration.
- **D14 Onboarding configuration:** Fresh 156-migration replay passed 42 suites / 1,070 assertions, including 109 Onboarding. The [raw database log](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/5fa75cf772d5a929408e97443b9cb005b4a71023/docs/stabilization/phase-08/d14-cutover-browser/isolated-sql.log.gz) preserves allocations and outcomes.
- **D15 Onboarding packets:** `20261001000200` reconciles packets atomically, `20261002000100` appends notes atomically and `20261002001100` starts packets atomically. The note proof passed fresh 161 migrations / 52 suites / 1,374 assertions and an 18-row / 71-assertion populated upgrade. Scheduled retention relies on the existing FK cascade without a migration. Robert reports all three migrations live.
- **D16 evaluator integrity:** `20260930000100` enforces same-Organization evaluator references through composite keys. The populated upgrade passed 13 rows / 26 assertions. Legacy foreign or mixed references can block Person or Organization deletion and retention, which rolls back instead of rewriting history. Robert reports it live.
- **D17 Organization destruction:** `20261002001000` grants the narrow owner Storage SELECT/DELETE (fresh 162 migrations / 56 suites / 1,441 assertions, populated 141-plus-21 / 17 suites / 271 assertions). `20261002002000`, SHA-256 `4d78c106db9bec79ff842e567aea570432e1ed984bfef4c51039136b5b25bd3d`, privately registers uploads and refuses deletion while an upload is unresolved. Its proof passed fresh 164 and populated 141-plus-23 migrations with 26 actual-session concurrency assertions. Robert reports both live.
- **D18 deletion requests:** `20261003000100` adds own-request cancellation. Fresh replay of 165 migrations includes 34 cancellation and 24 concurrency assertions, and the populated upgrade includes ten cancellation assertions. Migration 012 counseling atomicity remains; Storage ordering stays separate. Robert reports it live.
- **D20 communications:** New communications SQL passes 53 assertions within 157 migrations / 44 suites / 1,144 assertions, recorded in the [SQL receipt](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/5fa75cf772d5a929408e97443b9cb005b4a71023/docs/stabilization/phase-08/d20-communications-sql/receipt.json). No migration, policy or grant changed.
- **D21 platform membership:** `20261003000200`, SHA-256 `47f2b1070c9753527b862d39fa75fbcca65a9e633b810eb3bfeb97a1ac29009c`, adds Organization-owned atomic membership commands. Fresh 166 and populated 141-plus-25 migrations passed 84 pgTAP invocations / 2,033 assertions, including platform 47, concurrency 44 and upgrade 8. Robert reports it pushed.
- **D24 data export:** Focused isolated SQL passed 63 lifecycle and sensitive-read assertions without a migration.
- **D25 maintenance:** `20261004000200` adds the Critical Tasks retention RPC. Fresh replay and populated upgrade passed 88 invocations / 2,101 assertions, and a weak-lock negative control failed the intended oracle.
- **D26 progress scope:** `20261004000100` scopes Getting Started progress. Fresh replay passed 64 suites / 1,737 assertions and the populated upgrade 22 / 351. Robert reports it applied.
- **D27 access:** Policy, grant and context ownership reuse the #1527 isolated replay of unchanged schema, RLS and grants.
- **D28 Calendar removal:** `20261006000100` removes availability atomically through an invoker RPC. Fresh SQL and populated upgrade passed all retained contracts plus 33 and ten new removal checks. Robert reports it live at `2026-10-06T19:54:46.240Z`.

**Release obligations:** These remain open under [RELEASE](RELEASE.md) and the [D30 release list](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/5fa75cf772d5a929408e97443b9cb005b4a71023/docs/stabilization/phase-08/d30-integrated-convergence.md#release-obligations). The agent inspected no hosted state for them.

- D03 Onboarding `20260926000200` and Training `20260926000300` lack recorded owner-reported hosted application; their [confirmation obligation](RELEASE.md#phase-08-d03-onboarding-and-training-application-confirmation) remains open.
- The D12 Personnel retention RPC and the D25 Critical Tasks retention RPC still need a recorded owner-reported hosted result.
- D17 upload registration still needs production deployment and old-instance drain confirmation in the documented order.
- The `NOT VALID` Critical Tasks task-reference and Calendar status-reference constraints need a fresh private preflight count before any validation.
- Old-instance drain and normal-operation confirmations remain recorded for the D15, D18, D21 and D28 migrations.
- Repository CI performs no remote migration. Vercel may deploy on push or merge, and external deployment settings remain uninspected.

## Environment and commands

Original audit: `282fcace16795a13e2306bf71d44465f59d51acc`, September 16–17, 2026, Linux 7.2.3, 16 logical CPUs, 31,901,872 KiB RAM, Node 24.18.0/npm 11.12.1. Formatting, lint, governance, Svelte/types and synthetic production build passed. The full suite **failed** three date-sensitive invitation cases and one worker exited; the two-worker rerun still failed the same three cases. A billing-disabled probe also failed three SubscriptionBanner expectations. These are historical failures: invitation timing was repaired in 02-01 and clean-install flag isolation in 02-05; later passes do not change the original results. [Exact commands/results](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L5-L34).

The original tracked-source import inventory and migration-text schema counts are historical discovery, not a current effective catalog or comprehensive runtime proof. Current source enforcement is described under Phase 07; schema receipts below establish the local tested catalog. [Original method, limitations and ownership inventory](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L35-L73).

## Performance evidence

Historical production build at baseline `282fcace16795a13e2306bf71d44465f59d51acc` used billing false and unreachable synthetic provider settings. All client JS files: **290**, **4,727,769 bytes raw**, **1,421,898 bytes sum of individually gzip-compressed files**. Largest is ExcelJS `node_modules/exceljs/dist/exceljs.min.js`, emitted as `_app/immutable/chunks/DOle8-7O.js`: **929,913 raw / 254,430 gzip bytes**. These are build artifacts, not bytes transferred by any user journey.

Manifest diagnostic: following static `imports` from the page and known layout nodes (including root node 0), deduplicating emitted JS filenames, gives:

| Route                  | Node IDs     | JS files | Raw bytes | Sum gzip bytes |
| ---------------------- | ------------ | -------- | --------- | -------------- |
| Calendar               | 0, 4, 7, 49  | 97       | 676,643   | 225,022        |
| Personnel              | 0, 4, 11, 66 | 92       | 584,237   | 198,239        |
| Organization Dashboard | 0, 4, 6, 44  | 81       | 531,980   | 181,125        |
| Training reports       | 0, 4, 14, 86 | 101      | 713,803   | 234,542        |
| Critical Tasks reports | 0, 4, 8, 57  | 100      | 1,136,463 | 371,379        |

Method: resolve routes/nodes in `.svelte-kit/generated/client/app.js`, then traverse `.svelte-kit/output/client/.vite/manifest.json` entries `.svelte-kit/generated/client-optimized/nodes/<id>.js`. Sum file sizes and Python `gzip.compress` output per file. Excludes dynamic imports, app entry/runtime not reachable through those nodes, CSS/fonts, data/HTML, HTTP encoding/headers, caching and preload/navigation behavior. Node IDs/hashes apply only to this build. Thus these totals are static dependency diagnostics, **not complete cold-load or actual transferred-JS measurements**, and do not establish speed.

| Requested measurement                 | Calendar                                            | Personnel                                         | Dashboard                                  | Large lists / reports                            |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
| Network versus server time            | Not measured; server timing helper exists           | Not measured                                      | Not measured; summary/support timer exists | Not measured; Training timer exists              |
| HTML/data payload                     | Not measured                                        | Not measured                                      | Not measured                               | Not measured                                     |
| Actual transferred JS                 | Not measured; manifest diagnostic above             | Same                                              | Same                                       | Same                                             |
| Hydration / main-thread work          | Not measured                                        | Not measured                                      | Not measured                               | Not measured                                     |
| Repeat requests / interaction latency | Not measured; serial/deduplication guards inspected | Duplicate bootstrap read observed, timing unknown | Not measured                               | Unbounded source-load candidates, timing unknown |

No request, payload, hydration or interaction measurements were obtained in the original audit. These are Phase 12 inputs, not current performance claims.

## Phases 01–02: rules, safety coverage and E2E removal

**Accepted and merged in [PR #1289](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1289), candidate `eb43ff9be78210cf438227bfdff4b79c9209ee2f`.** The source-backed [Phase 03 prerequisite verification](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L1525-L1538) reconciled the final PR tree and checks. Rules/verification were consolidated; auth/session/MFA/password/revocation, private-resource, invitation/setup/provisioning and retained Calendar contracts gained meaningful protection. E2E runtime bypasses, app-owned email test transport, all 33 harness files, seven scripts and direct runner dependencies were removed after retained-contract transfer. Clean installation without E2E values passed. [Full accepted task records](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L100-L1538) and [completed task map](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/TODO.md#L20-L126).

Final merged candidate: all seven canonical commands (`format`, `format:check`, `lint`, `governance:check`, `check`, `test`, `build`) passed; **615 files / 5,723 tests**, zero Svelte/type diagnostics. The final Calendar Group-ID conversion passed isolated fresh and populated-upgrade **18 SQL suites / 347 assertions** plus **nine actual migration assertions** for conversion, preservation, idempotence and atomic refusal. Browser evidence covered renamed daily/monthly Group selection, save/reload, allowed Group access and denied cross-scope writes. An attempt to replace an invisible Bravo slot with Alpha returned generic **500**, not 403; the saved row was unchanged. [Final Group repair and release requirement](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L1489-L1524).

The preceding persistence proof exercised real setup/invitation one-winner concurrency, expiry/replay, JWT identity, denied roles, owner/membership rollback and Calendar tenant/Group RLS. Removing the setup claim lock caused the expected failure. The pre-conversion upgrade preserved **27 complete rows** exactly, hash `c58988cbf8d5179773c2a0e5f13a5ad2526ce73183c31c4fd291501c180cc559`; fresh SQL then passed **18 suites / 336 assertions**. These earlier totals are separate from final conversion coverage. [Security, fixture and isolated environment receipt](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L1344-L1376).

The Calendar synthetic fixture contract requires saved IDs, dates, type/assignee/scope, manual and edited-generated facts, referenced types, availability/status, rank, Duty Role and independent Sign-In Rosters to survive later removal. Phase 06 applies that contract; no regeneration from retired history or name-based type deletion is authorized. [Original fixture specification](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L1271-L1286).

**Limits retained:** F01 invitation approval provenance, F02 atomic slot replacement and F06 recoverable Organization bootstrap remain Phase 11 work. The broad assertion-quality audit remains 13-03. Phase 15 still owns current production-mode Calendar legend/overflow, Personnel/import reload, Training save/error/retry, Onboarding persistence, settings/direct-route denial and page-arrival/hydration observations; earlier scoped observations do not certify every later release. [Contract transfer and external-flow obligations](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L1310-L1343).
