## Unresolved findings and evidence limits

- **Setup recovery follow-up:** [CodeRabbit's PR #1380 finding](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/pull/1380#discussion_r4110340137) was intentionally left for [#1381](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1381). The separate [#1381 acceptance](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/5fa75cf772d5a929408e97443b9cb005b4a71023/docs/stabilization/phase-08/1381-account-setup-recovery.md), merged through #1382 as `af29a82e1bcc2ae0efc9a42c924ea120c2760092`, gives valid same-email links a fenced retry or committed-write recovery and browser guidance. Historical claims without a transaction witness still require staff. The #1380 finding remains historical evidence of its merged state, not a claim that #1380 itself contained the fix.
- **Module migration debt:** Phase 07 closed with 1,695 findings, and D01, D02 and D03 retained 1,596, 1,537 and 1,292. Fresh `npm run governance:check` at `8072a9ef` reports 0 findings and now fails on any finding. F01 approval provenance, F06 recoverable Organization bootstrap, F07 local CSP, mapping/error/state work, the unused `is_full_editor_for_org` helper, legacy seed provenance and residual test audit keep their owners in TODO. Phase 11-02 already delivered the F02 transactional assignment prerequisite. Removing obsolete documentation resolves none of the remaining items.
- **Phase 08 hosted state:** Hosted migration applications recorded here are owner-reported. D03 Onboarding `20260926000200` and Training `20260926000300` remain unconfirmed in RELEASE. No agent read a hosted ledger, so hosted schema and policy parity remain unverified. The Phase 08 section lists the open release obligations.
- **Phase 08 nontransactional limits:** Counseling Storage removal precedes and lies outside the counseling RPC (D03, D18). Training type approval keeps effect-before-status ordering, so a failed status write leaves a pending request with a missing type (D08). Artifact retention keeps database-before-Storage partial effects, possible orphan files and the ID-based child restore race (D13). Onboarding template reorder and duplicate keep multiwrite behavior (D14). D18 existence lookup and insertion stay separate, so concurrent empty reads can both insert, and a global index needs a separate product and schema decision. Direct Calendar assignment writers do not take the D06 advisory lock.
- **Phase 08 owner-deferred observations:** The D28 Calendar baseline keeps two rare edges as [#1554](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1554) and [#1555](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1555). Its final native run closed with 102 PASS, one nonblocking ISSUE and 13 UNRUN after owner scope adjustment. [#1557](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1557) keeps the malformed batch-date HTTP 500 and [#1559](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1559) the optional single-Person scoped-read optimization; the [Calendar record](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/5fa75cf772d5a929408e97443b9cb005b4a71023/docs/stabilization/phase-08/d28-calendar-owned-persistence.md) owns their detail. [#1321](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1321) keeps Group reassignment ID churn. These are open backlog, not Phase 08 blockers.
- **Phase 08 unrun and unverified paths:** Hosted email delivery, MFA enrollment and the one-off legacy sign-in 503 (D02), new-account setup-link completion and controlled live transfer failure (D05), and real Resend, GitHub and mail delivery (D01, D20) were not exercised. Native default expiry (D28 residual settings), native nullable dates (D28 Onboarding) and one counseling Open step (D29) stay UNRUN. External Units callers and hosted telemetry are uninspected (D17).
- **Phase 08 unresolved causes:** The D28 preparation counseling reload Error 500 did not reproduce later, and its cause stays unknown. D04's first failed availability-lifecycle attempt passed on unchanged rerun without an established cause. D13's `/tmp` inode exhaustion has no proven root cause. Vitest worker exits in D04 and D05 and a load-induced D29 timeout are not explained by later passing runs. Whole-machine resource equality failed in the D28 residual settings run for recorded unowned changes.
- **Phase 08 privacy and product limits:** D28 Personnel and Onboarding records disclose synthetic-token exposure in tool transcripts. Published archives replace real signed-token values with recorded placeholders. D10 duplicate-code edits still show the shared store's generic save error. D13 dedicated pages can keep the prior Organization's browser title. The D20 logged-empty Feedback list failure stays under the preserve-error contract.
- **Phase 10 D01 observations:** the stale password-attempt message after a completed password change is identity attempt policy recorded for Phase 11. The Leave Organization dialog under a re-portaled 390 drawer and the announcement banner above modal overlays predate D01 and are repaired by `e30df54a`. Margins on full-width `Empty.Root` in the Calendar and Training in-page Access Restricted branches remain open for D03 [#1601](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1601) and D04 [#1602](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/issues/1602). Details are in [Phase 10 D01](#phase-10-d01-1599).
- **Release observations:** Phase 15 must collect current critical-journey and performance evidence. #1315's unavailable hydrated-browser observations, earlier interrupted recordings and unobserved flows are not passes. Current operator obligations and owner-report distinctions live in RELEASE and schema preflights.
- **Local isolation incident:** a September 18 SQL rerun was mistakenly launched from the repository and reached the existing development database. Its first transactional setup-claim test triggered a PostgreSQL backend segfault; recovery rolled back uncommitted fixture writes. No reset, migration or remote database operation ran there. Only status/log inspection followed, then verification used explicitly disposable directories. This was an execution error, not an untouched-local-stack claim. [Committed incident receipt](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L1489-L1512).
- **Historical SQL tooling failure:** the initial isolated PostgreSQL image crashed on reserved-role denial (`supautils.so`). The final 02-03-04 proof disabled only hint decoration inside its disposable container; actual `42501` denial assertions and all grants/RLS remained. Initial crash runs failed. [Exact adjustment and limits](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L1361-L1376).
- **Intermittent test termination:** worker exits, observed SIGKILL, host-killed publication attempts and EPIPE failures are not fixed by later passing runs. Owner-directed Phase 04 review stopped the unrelated signal-attribution investigation after no implementation defect was found; no Tailscale/T3/remote-session cause was established. [Failure, diagnosis and final disposition](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L3016-L3092). No investigation restart is queued by this cleanup.
- **External browser tooling:** T3's captured optional 500 ms metadata timeout disconnected its automation host; the slow-response cause and deterministic reproduction remained unresolved. [Recorded diagnosis and upstream report boundary](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L514-L525). Completed later app-flow observations do not establish a T3 repair.
- **Warnings/advisories:** historical unused `waitFor`, bundle-size/mixed-import and optional OpenTelemetry warnings remain observations, not suppressed checks. The clean-install audit reported **18 findings (2 low / 9 moderate / 6 high / 1 critical)** at that time; no remediation or current vulnerability count is inferred. [Install and environment receipt](https://github.com/waffleflopper/trooptotask/blob/dbd501301df350ef4e6cebb6bc1238283b24d9c9/docs/stabilization/EVIDENCE.md#L1425-L1433).

Future updates replace the current acceptance summary when its inputs change, consolidate resolved attempts into committed references, and keep failed/unavailable results with their final disposition. Preserve security/schema proof and release obligations; do not append another work diary or claim a historical run verifies changed inputs.
